# Layer changelog

Source: https://core.telegram.org/api/layers

Below you will find information on schema changes. For more details on the use of layers, see [Invoking API methods](https://core.telegram.org/api/invoking#layers).

### [Layer 225](https://core.telegram.org/schema?layer=225)

To view all changes since the last documentation refresh, start reading at [layer 215](https://core.telegram.org/api/layers#layer-215).

This documentation release includes the following new and substantially overhauled articles, documenting pre-existing features:

* [Group calls, video chats, live stories, conferences and livestreams »](https://core.telegram.org/api/group-calls)
* [One-to-one calls »](https://core.telegram.org/api/calls)
* [Completely overhauled the E2E conference documentation »](https://core.telegram.org/api/end-to-end/group-calls)
* [Completely overhauled the file reference database schema, making it significantly easier to autogenerate the file reference database »](https://core.telegram.org/api/file-references)
* [Applying basic group updates »](https://core.telegram.org/api/peers#basic-group-updates)
* [Applying group call updates »](https://core.telegram.org/api/end-to-end/group-calls#handling-updates)
* [Message ID sequences »](https://core.telegram.org/api/updates#message-id-sequences)
* [More detailed instructions for update gap handling (also considering gaps beyond the max message box size) »](https://core.telegram.org/api/updates#recovering-gaps-for-very-old-messages)
* [Parallel sessions »](https://core.telegram.org/api/datacenter#parallel-sessions)
* [updateMessageID handling »](https://core.telegram.org/api/updates#updatemessageid-updates)
* [Documented rate limits for live drafts »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/ai#live-response-streaming)
* [Content protection »](https://core.telegram.org/api/content-protection)
* [Message views »](https://core.telegram.org/api/views)
* [Sending messages on behalf of owned channels and groups »](https://core.telegram.org/api/channel#sending-messages-on-behalf-of-owned-channels-and-groups)
* [Working with basic group participants »](https://core.telegram.org/api/channel#basic-group-participants)
* [Leaving groups/channels (full flow) »](https://core.telegram.org/api/channel#leaving-groups-channels)
* [Added the layer number to the RPC error DB »](https://core.telegram.org/api/errors)
* [Improved the takeout documentation »](https://core.telegram.org/api/takeout)
* Documented the [`navigationBarColor` »](https://core.telegram.org/api/web-events#navigationbarcolor), [`actionBarColor` »](https://core.telegram.org/api/web-events#actionbarcolor), [`allowScroll` »](https://core.telegram.org/api/web-events#allowscroll), [`siteName` »](https://core.telegram.org/api/web-events#sitename) event types
* [`whitelisted_bots` »](https://core.telegram.org/api/config#whitelisted-bots)
* Detailed changelogs for API configuration keys, web events, limits, suggested actions and other API constants will now be automatically listed in the appropriate layer changelog, instead of the latest layer.
* Published an aggregated, machine-readable index of all [API constants and push updates »](https://core.telegram.org/api/config#machine-readable-configuration-index) at the new [/api/config.json »](https://core.telegram.org/api/config.json) permalink.
* The [RPC error database »](https://core.telegram.org/api/errors#error-database) is now also published as a machine-readable [/api/errors.json »](https://core.telegram.org/api/errors.json) permalink, replacing the previous embedded file link.
* Multiple other improvements and fixes across the board.

And documenting the following new features (a brief overview of features introduced since [layer 215](https://core.telegram.org/api/layers#layer-215)):

* AI features: [message composition »](https://core.telegram.org/api/ai#compose-messages), [AI compose tones »](https://core.telegram.org/api/ai#ai-compose-tones) and [message summarization »](https://core.telegram.org/api/ai#summarize-messages)
* Bot AI features: [live drafts »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/ai#live-response-streaming)
* [Bot forums »](https://core.telegram.org/api/forum#bot-forums)
* [Managed bots »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/managed-bots)
* [Improved styled text entities »](https://core.telegram.org/api/entities)
* [Guest mode for bots »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/guest-mode)
* [Gift auctions »](https://core.telegram.org/api/auctions)
* [Passkeys »](https://core.telegram.org/api/passkeys)
* [Live stories »](https://core.telegram.org/api/group-calls#live-stories), [in-call messages »](https://core.telegram.org/api/group-calls#in-call-messages), [paid live story comments »](https://core.telegram.org/api/group-calls#paid-live-story-comments) and [paid live story donations »](https://core.telegram.org/api/group-calls#paid-live-story-donations)
* [Recent story summaries »](https://core.telegram.org/api/stories#recent-story-summaries)
* [Repeating scheduled messages »](https://core.telegram.org/api/scheduled-messages#repeating-scheduled-messages)
* [Message views »](https://core.telegram.org/api/views#view-counters) and [read metrics »](https://core.telegram.org/api/views#read-metrics)
* [Open-answer polls »](https://core.telegram.org/api/poll#open-answer-polls), [unread poll votes »](https://core.telegram.org/api/poll#unread-poll-votes), [poll answer media attachments »](https://core.telegram.org/api/poll#sending-a-poll), [replies to poll options »](https://core.telegram.org/api/poll#replying-to-poll-options), [poll search »](https://core.telegram.org/api/poll#searching-for-polls) and [poll statistics »](https://core.telegram.org/api/stats#poll-statistics)
* [Group member/admin tags »](https://core.telegram.org/api/rank)
* [Group/channel ownership transfer »](https://core.telegram.org/api/channel#transferring-ownership-of-a-group-channel) and [leave handoff flows »](https://core.telegram.org/api/channel#leaving-groups-channels)
* [Date styled text entities »](https://core.telegram.org/api/entities#date-entities) and [diff styled text entities »](https://core.telegram.org/api/entities#diff-entities)
* [Collectible gifts »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gifts): [crafting »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#crafting-collectible-gifts), [purchase offers »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gift-purchase-offers), [hosted gifts »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#hosted-collectible-gifts), [collectible message palettes »](https://core.telegram.org/api/colors#collectible-message-palettes) and [collectible profile palettes »](https://core.telegram.org/api/colors#collectible-profile-palettes).
* [Private contact notes »](https://core.telegram.org/api/contacts#private-notes-for-contacts)
* [Birthdays »](https://core.telegram.org/api/profile#birthday), [music profile tab »](https://core.telegram.org/api/profile#music), [managed bot manager info »](https://core.telegram.org/api/profile#managed-bot-manager), [personal channels »](https://core.telegram.org/api/profile#personal-channel) and [unofficial client warnings »](https://core.telegram.org/api/profile#unofficial-client-warning)
* [Private chat content protection »](https://core.telegram.org/api/content-protection#for-users)
* [Button styles »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/buttons#button-styles)
* [Peer requests for Mini Apps »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/buttons#requesting-peers-via-mini-apps)
* [Anonymous group admins »](https://core.telegram.org/api/rights#anonymous-admins) can now [toggle checklist items »](https://core.telegram.org/api/todo#mark-items-as-completed)
* [Removing participant reactions »](https://core.telegram.org/api/reactions#removing-participant-reactions)
* [Message effects »](https://core.telegram.org/api/effects) support for forwarded messages
* [Login email setup suggestions »](https://core.telegram.org/api/auth#email-verification) and [passkey setup suggestions »](https://core.telegram.org/api/config#setup-passkey)
* [OAuth login »](https://core.telegram.org/api/url-authorization)
* [`blur_enabled` in `fullscreen_changed` events »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/webapps#fullscreen-changed)
* New [deep link »](https://core.telegram.org/api/links) types:
  + [Parameterized settings deep links, with support for opening a specific settings page or subsection »](https://core.telegram.org/api/links#settings-links)
  + [Contact list deep links »](https://core.telegram.org/api/links#contacts-links)
  + [Chat creation deep links »](https://core.telegram.org/api/links#new-chat-links)
  + [Chat list deep links »](https://core.telegram.org/api/links#chat-list-links)
  + [Live story deep links »](https://core.telegram.org/api/links#live-story-links)
  + [Story composer deep links »](https://core.telegram.org/api/links#post-story-links)
  + [Timestamp links »](https://core.telegram.org/api/links#timestamp-links)
  + [Highlighting of a specific checklist task or poll option in message deep links »](https://core.telegram.org/api/links#message-links)

This layer introduces the following features:

* [Custom AI compose tones »](https://core.telegram.org/api/ai#ai-compose-tones)
* [Guest mode for bots »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/guest-mode)
* [Poll statistics »](https://core.telegram.org/api/stats#poll-statistics)
* [Participant reaction moderation »](https://core.telegram.org/api/reactions#removing-participant-reactions)
* [Allowed bots to fetch the history of the personal channel of any user »](https://core.telegram.org/api/profile#personal-channel)

#### API constants

##### Premium limits

[Telegram Premium limit identifiers »](https://core.telegram.org/api/premium#double-limits).

* Added [`double_limits__aicompose_tone_saved` »](https://core.telegram.org/api/premium#double-limits-aicompose-tone-saved)

##### Web page type values

Web page type values from the [webPage](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/webPage) constructor.

* Added `telegram_aicomposetone` - Preview of an [AI compose tone link »](https://core.telegram.org/api/links#ai-compose-tone-links), shared when previewing a [custom AI composer tone »](https://core.telegram.org/api/ai#ai-compose-tones). The `attributes` field contains a [webPageAttributeAiComposeTone](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/webPageAttributeAiComposeTone) with the [custom emoji »](https://core.telegram.org/api/custom-emoji) representing the tone; clients render a button that applies the shared tone.

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [messages.setBotGuestChatResult](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.setBotGuestChatResult) - Bots may use this method to answer a [guest mode »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/guest-mode) query received via an [updateBotGuestChatQuery](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updateBotGuestChatQuery) update, providing the message to post into the chat as a guest, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/guest-mode#handling-guest-queries-bot-side) for more info.
* Added [messages.deleteParticipantReactions](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.deleteParticipantReactions) - As an admin, remove all of a specific participant's [reactions](https://core.telegram.org/api/reactions) from every message in a group or channel.
* Added [messages.deleteParticipantReaction](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.deleteParticipantReaction) - As an admin, remove all of a specific participant's [reactions](https://core.telegram.org/api/reactions) from a single message.
* Added [messages.getPersonalChannelHistory](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.getPersonalChannelHistory) - Fetch the message history of a user's [personal channel »](https://core.telegram.org/api/profile#personal-channel).
* Added [bots.getAccessSettings](https://core.telegram.org/method/bots.getAccessSettings) - Get the [access restriction settings »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/managed-bots#managing-a-managed-bot) of a managed bot; can only be called by the manager bot.
* Added [bots.editAccessSettings](https://core.telegram.org/method/bots.editAccessSettings) - Edit the [access restriction settings »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/managed-bots#managing-a-managed-bot) of a managed bot; can only be called by the manager bot.
* Added [stats.getPollStats](https://core.telegram.org/method/stats.getPollStats) - Get [statistics](https://core.telegram.org/api/stats#poll-statistics) for a poll sent in a message.
* Added [aicompose.createTone](https://core.telegram.org/method/aicompose.createTone) - Create a new custom [AI composer tone »](https://core.telegram.org/api/ai#ai-compose-tones).
* Added [aicompose.updateTone](https://core.telegram.org/method/aicompose.updateTone) - Edit a custom [AI composer tone »](https://core.telegram.org/api/ai#ai-compose-tones) previously created by the current user. Only the fields whose flag is set will be modified.
* Added [aicompose.saveTone](https://core.telegram.org/method/aicompose.saveTone) - Install or uninstall an [AI composer tone »](https://core.telegram.org/api/ai#ai-compose-tones), adding it to or removing it from the list of saved tones of the current user.
* Added [aicompose.deleteTone](https://core.telegram.org/method/aicompose.deleteTone) - Permanently delete a custom [AI composer tone »](https://core.telegram.org/api/ai#ai-compose-tones) created by the current user.
* Added [aicompose.getTone](https://core.telegram.org/method/aicompose.getTone) - Fetch information about a single [AI composer tone »](https://core.telegram.org/api/ai#ai-compose-tones), for example to resolve a shared tone deep link.
* Added [aicompose.getTones](https://core.telegram.org/method/aicompose.getTones) - Fetch the list of saved [AI composer tones »](https://core.telegram.org/api/ai#ai-compose-tones) of the current user.
* Added [aicompose.getToneExample](https://core.telegram.org/method/aicompose.getToneExample) - Fetch an example showing how an [AI composer tone »](https://core.telegram.org/api/ai#ai-compose-tones) rephrases a sample message, used as a preview in the tone picker.

##### Changed Methods

* Added **bots_guestchat** parameter in [contacts.getTopPeers](https://core.telegram.org/method/contacts.getTopPeers)
* Added **tone** parameter, removed **change_tone** parameter in [messages.composeMessageWithAI](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.composeMessageWithAI)

##### New Constructors

* Added [updateBotGuestChatQuery](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updateBotGuestChatQuery) - Sent to [guest bots »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/guest-mode) when a user invokes the bot as a guest in a chat. The bot should reply by invoking [messages.setBotGuestChatResult](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.setBotGuestChatResult), see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/guest-mode#handling-guest-queries-bot-side) for more info.
* Added [updateAiComposeTones](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updateAiComposeTones) - The list of saved [AI composer tones »](https://core.telegram.org/api/ai#ai-compose-tones) of the current user has changed, and should be refetched using [aicompose.getTones](https://core.telegram.org/method/aicompose.getTones).
* Added [topPeerCategoryBotsGuestChat](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/topPeerCategoryBotsGuestChat) - Top [guest bots »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/guest-mode), i.e. bots most frequently invoked as guests in chats.
* Added [webPageAttributeAiComposeTone](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/webPageAttributeAiComposeTone) - [Webpage attribute](https://core.telegram.org/type/WebPageAttribute) attached to a [webPage](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/webPage) of type `telegram_aicomposetone`, generated when previewing a shared [AI composer tone »](https://core.telegram.org/api/ai#ai-compose-tones) via an [AI compose tone link »](https://core.telegram.org/api/links#ai-compose-tone-links).
* Added [stats.pollStats](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/stats.pollStats) - [Statistics](https://core.telegram.org/api/stats#poll-statistics) for a poll sent in a message.
* Added [inputAiComposeToneDefault](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputAiComposeToneDefault) - References a built-in, default [AI composer tone »](https://core.telegram.org/api/ai#ai-compose-tones) by its string identifier.
* Added [inputAiComposeToneID](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputAiComposeToneID) - References a custom [AI composer tone »](https://core.telegram.org/api/ai#ai-compose-tones) by its ID and access hash.
* Added [inputAiComposeToneSlug](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputAiComposeToneSlug) - References a custom [AI composer tone »](https://core.telegram.org/api/ai#ai-compose-tones) by its public slug, used when opening an [AI compose tone link »](https://core.telegram.org/api/links#ai-compose-tone-links).
* Added [aiComposeTone](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/aiComposeTone) - A custom [AI composer tone »](https://core.telegram.org/api/ai#ai-compose-tones), used to rephrase messages in a specific style with the [AI message composer](https://core.telegram.org/api/ai#compose-messages).
* Added [aiComposeToneDefault](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/aiComposeToneDefault) - A built-in, default [AI composer tone »](https://core.telegram.org/api/ai#ai-compose-tones), identified by a string identifier rather than by a numeric ID.
* Added [aicompose.tonesNotModified](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/aicompose.tonesNotModified) - The list of saved [AI composer tones »](https://core.telegram.org/api/ai#ai-compose-tones) hasn't changed since the last time it was fetched (i.e. the `hash` passed to [aicompose.getTones](https://core.telegram.org/method/aicompose.getTones) is still valid).
* Added [aicompose.tones](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/aicompose.tones) - The list of saved [AI composer tones »](https://core.telegram.org/api/ai#ai-compose-tones) of the current user.
* Added [aiComposeToneExample](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/aiComposeToneExample) - An example showing how an [AI composer tone »](https://core.telegram.org/api/ai#ai-compose-tones) rephrases a message, used as a preview in the tone picker.
* Added [bots.accessSettings](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/bots.accessSettings) - Access restriction settings for a [managed bot »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/managed-bots#managing-a-managed-bot).

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **bot_guestchat** parameter in [user](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/user)
* Added **guestchat_via_from** parameter in [message](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/message)
* Added **premium_days** parameter in [auth.sentCodePaymentRequired](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/auth.sentCodePaymentRequired)
* Added **subscribers_only**, **countries_iso2** parameters in [poll](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/poll)
* Added **can_view_stats** parameter in [pollResults](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/pollResults)
* Added **send_reactions** parameter in [chatBannedRights](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/chatBannedRights)
* Added **premium_days** parameter in [inputStorePaymentAuthCode](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputStorePaymentAuthCode)

#### [Changes in the file reference database map file »](https://core.telegram.org/api/file-references)

##### New traversers incoming

* Added

  ```
  traverseIncomingConstructor{predicate: updateBotGuestChatQuery, params: [message, reference_messages], type: Update}
  ```
* Added

  ```
  traverseMethodResult{name: messages.getPersonalChannelHistory}
  ```

##### New traversers outgoing

* Added

  ```
  traverseMethodCall{name: messages.setBotGuestChatResult, params: [result]}
  ```

### [Layer 224](https://core.telegram.org/schema?layer=224)

This layer introduces the following features:

* [AI message editor »](https://core.telegram.org/api/ai#compose-messages)
* [Diff styled text entities »](https://core.telegram.org/api/entities#diff-entities)
* [Message views and read metrics »](https://core.telegram.org/api/views)
* [Managed bots »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/managed-bots)
* [Managed bot manager info »](https://core.telegram.org/api/profile#managed-bot-manager)
* [Peer requests for mini apps »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/buttons#requesting-peers-via-mini-apps)
* [Detailed message metrics »](https://core.telegram.org/api/views#read-metrics)
* [Open-answer polls »](https://core.telegram.org/api/poll#open-answer-polls)
* [Unread poll votes »](https://core.telegram.org/api/poll#unread-poll-votes)
* [Poll answer media attachments »](https://core.telegram.org/api/poll#sending-a-poll)
* [Replies to poll options »](https://core.telegram.org/api/poll#replying-to-poll-options)
* [Search for poll messages »](https://core.telegram.org/api/poll#searching-for-polls)
* [Unofficial client warning »](https://core.telegram.org/api/profile#unofficial-client-warning)

#### API constants

##### Incoming web events

Incoming web events documented [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/web-events#event-types).

* Added [`web_app_request_chat` »](https://core.telegram.org/api/web-events#web-app-request-chat)

##### Mini app outgoing events

Outgoing mini app events documented [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/webapps#incoming-events-client-to-mini-app).

* Added [`requested_chat_failed` »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/webapps#requested-chat-failed)
* Added [`requested_chat_sent` »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/webapps#requested-chat-sent)

##### Premium features

[Telegram Premium feature identifiers »](https://core.telegram.org/api/premium#telegram-premium-features).

* Added [`ai_compose` »](https://core.telegram.org/api/premium#ai-compose)

##### Premium limits

[Telegram Premium limit identifiers »](https://core.telegram.org/api/premium#double-limits).

* Added [`double_limits__bots_create` »](https://core.telegram.org/api/premium#double-limits-bots-create)

##### Web page type values

Web page type values from the [webPage](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/webPage) constructor.

* Added `telegram_newbot`

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [messages.composeMessageWithAI](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.composeMessageWithAI) - Invokes telegram's AI Editor that can translate, transform, fixup and/or emojify your message in a number of different ways, privately powered by [Cocoon](https://cocoon.org), see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/ai#compose-messages) for more info!
* Added [messages.reportReadMetrics](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.reportReadMetrics) - Report viewport read metrics for visible messages, indicating how long each message stayed in the chat viewport, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/views#read-metrics) for more info on the full flow.
* Added [messages.reportMusicListen](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.reportMusicListen) - Report the listening duration of a music track (audio document without the `voice` flag), see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/views#music-listens) for more info on the full flow.
* Added [messages.addPollAnswer](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.addPollAnswer) - Add an answer option to an [open-answer poll »](https://core.telegram.org/api/poll#open-answer-polls)
* Added [messages.deletePollAnswer](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.deletePollAnswer) - Remove an answer option from an [open-answer poll »](https://core.telegram.org/api/poll#open-answer-polls)
* Added [messages.getUnreadPollVotes](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.getUnreadPollVotes) - Get messages containing polls with [unread votes »](https://core.telegram.org/api/poll#unread-poll-votes)
* Added [messages.readPollVotes](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.readPollVotes) - Mark all [unread poll votes »](https://core.telegram.org/api/poll#unread-poll-votes) in a chat as read
* Added [bots.checkUsername](https://core.telegram.org/method/bots.checkUsername) - Check whether a username is available and valid for use when [creating a managed bot »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/managed-bots#creating-a-managed-bot).
* Added [bots.createBot](https://core.telegram.org/method/bots.createBot) - Create a [managed bot »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/managed-bots#creating-a-managed-bot) owned by the current user and controlled by the specified manager bot.
* Added [bots.exportBotToken](https://core.telegram.org/method/bots.exportBotToken) - Export the bot token of a [managed bot »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/managed-bots#managing-a-managed-bot); can only be called by the manager bot.
* Added [bots.requestWebViewButton](https://core.telegram.org/method/bots.requestWebViewButton) - Bots may use this method to prepare a peer request button for a [Mini App](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/webapps), see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/buttons#requesting-peers-via-mini-apps) for more info.
* Added [bots.getRequestedWebViewButton](https://core.telegram.org/method/bots.getRequestedWebViewButton) - Fetch the peer request button a bot prepared for a [Mini App](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/webapps) with [bots.requestWebViewButton](https://core.telegram.org/method/bots.requestWebViewButton), invoked when the Mini App emits a [`web_app_request_chat`](https://core.telegram.org/api/web-events#web-app-request-chat) event, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/buttons#requesting-peers-via-mini-apps) for more info.

##### Changed Methods

* Added **poll_hash** parameter in [messages.getPollResults](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.getPollResults)
* Added **tone** parameter in [messages.translateText](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.translateText)
* Added **flags**, **webapp_req_id** parameters, changed type of **msg_id** from **int** to **flags.0?int** in [messages.sendBotRequestedPeer](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.sendBotRequestedPeer)
* Added **tone** parameter in [messages.summarizeText](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.summarizeText)
* Added **music** parameter in [stories.sendStory](https://core.telegram.org/method/stories.sendStory)
* Added **music** parameter in [stories.editStory](https://core.telegram.org/method/stories.editStory)

##### New Constructors

* Added [messageActionPollAppendAnswer](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionPollAppendAnswer) - A new answer option was added to an [open-answer poll »](https://core.telegram.org/api/poll#open-answer-polls)
* Added [messageActionPollDeleteAnswer](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionPollDeleteAnswer) - An answer option was removed from an [open-answer poll »](https://core.telegram.org/api/poll#open-answer-polls)
* Added [messageActionManagedBotCreated](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionManagedBotCreated) - Service message sent by a user to a manager bot when a new [managed bot »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/managed-bots) is created through it, by invoking [messages.sendBotRequestedPeer](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.sendBotRequestedPeer) with a [requestPeerTypeCreateBot](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/requestPeerTypeCreateBot), see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/buttons#requesting-a-managed-bot) for more info.
* Added [inputMessagesFilterPoll](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputMessagesFilterPoll) - Filter for poll messages, see [searching for polls »](https://core.telegram.org/api/poll#searching-for-polls)
* Added [updateManagedBot](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updateManagedBot) - [Manager bots](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/managed-bots) only: a bot managed by the currently logged in bot was created or updated.
* Added [messageEntityDiffInsert](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageEntityDiffInsert) - Represents an diff addition: render it by simply underlining the specified section and coloring it in green, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/entities#diff-entities) for more info on how to render diff entities.
* Added [messageEntityDiffReplace](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageEntityDiffReplace) - Represents an diff replacement, render it as follows:
* Added [messageEntityDiffDelete](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageEntityDiffDelete) - Represents an diff deletion: render it by simply underlining the specified section and coloring it in red, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/entities#diff-entities) for more info on how to render diff entities.
* Added [inputPollAnswer](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputPollAnswer) - An answer option to add to an [open-answer poll »](https://core.telegram.org/api/poll#open-answer-polls)
* Added [requestPeerTypeCreateBot](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/requestPeerTypeCreateBot) - Used in a [keyboardButtonRequestPeer](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/keyboardButtonRequestPeer) by a [manager bot](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/managed-bots) to ask a user to create a new [managed bot »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/managed-bots), see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/buttons#requesting-a-managed-bot) for more info.
* Added [inputMessageReadMetric](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputMessageReadMetric) - Read metric for a single message exposure, describing how long the message was visible in the chat viewport.
* Added [bots.exportedBotToken](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/bots.exportedBotToken) - The bot token of a [managed bot »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/managed-bots#managing-a-managed-bot), exported by the manager bot.
* Added [bots.requestedButton](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/bots.requestedButton) - Contains the request ID a bot should pass to a [Mini App](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/webapps) after preparing a peer request button with [bots.requestWebViewButton](https://core.telegram.org/method/bots.requestWebViewButton), see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/buttons#requesting-peers-via-mini-apps) for more info.
* Added [messages.composedMessageWithAI](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messages.composedMessageWithAI) - Represents a message modified by AI.

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **live_photo**, **video** parameters in [inputMediaUploadedPhoto](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputMediaUploadedPhoto)
* Added **live_photo**, **video** parameters in [inputMediaPhoto](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputMediaPhoto)
* Added **attached_media**, **solution_media** parameters, changed type of **correct_answers** from **flags.0?Vector<bytes>** to **flags.0?Vector<int>** in [inputMediaPoll](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputMediaPoll)
* Added **bot_can_manage_bots** parameter in [user](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/user)
* Added **live_photo**, **video** parameters in [messageMediaPhoto](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageMediaPhoto)
* Added **flags**, **attached_media** parameters in [messageMediaPoll](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageMediaPoll)
* Added **unread_poll_votes_count** parameter in [dialog](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/dialog)
* Added **unofficial_security_risk**, **bot_manager_id** parameters in [userFull](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/userFull)
* Added **peer**, **msg_id**, **top_msg_id** parameters in [updateMessagePoll](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updateMessagePoll)
* Added **positions** parameter in [updateMessagePollVote](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updateMessagePollVote)
* Added **flags**, **media**, **added_by**, **date** parameters in [pollAnswer](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/pollAnswer)
* Added **open_answers**, **revoting_disabled**, **shuffle_answers**, **hide_results_until_close**, **creator**, **hash** parameters in [poll](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/poll)
* Added **recent_voters** parameter, changed type of **voters** from **int** to **flags.2?int** in [pollAnswerVoters](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/pollAnswerVoters)
* Added **has_unread_votes**, **solution_media** parameters in [pollResults](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/pollResults)
* Added **is_app**, **verified_app_name** parameters in [urlAuthResultRequest](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/urlAuthResultRequest)
* Added **poll_option** parameter in [messageReplyHeader](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageReplyHeader)
* Added **unread_poll_votes_count** parameter in [forumTopic](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/forumTopic)
* Added **music** parameter in [storyItem](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/storyItem)
* Added **poll_option** parameter in [inputReplyToMessage](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputReplyToMessage)
* Added **poll_votes_notify_from** parameter in [reactionsNotifySettings](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/reactionsNotifySettings)

#### [Changes in the file reference database map file »](https://core.telegram.org/api/file-references)

##### New sources

* Added [updateMessagePoll](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updateMessagePoll) -

  ```
  (unless messages.getScheduledMessages, updateNewScheduledMessage) fileSourceMessage{peer: extractPeerIdFromPeerAndStore(updateMessagePoll.peer?abort_if_empty), id: updateMessagePoll.msg_id?abort_if_empty, quick_reply_shortcut_id: false}
  ```

##### New traversers incoming

* Added

  ```
  traverseIncomingConstructor{predicate: messageActionPollAppendAnswer, params: [answer], type: MessageAction}
  ```
* Added

  ```
  traverseIncomingConstructor{predicate: messageActionPollDeleteAnswer, params: [answer], type: MessageAction}
  ```
* Added

  ```
  traverseIncomingConstructor{predicate: messageMediaPoll, params: [poll, results, attached_media], type: MessageMedia}
  ```
* Added

  ```
  traverseIncomingConstructor{predicate: poll, params: [answers], type: Poll}
  ```
* Added

  ```
  traverseIncomingConstructor{predicate: pollAnswer, params: [media], type: PollAnswer}
  ```
* Added

  ```
  traverseIncomingConstructor{predicate: pollResults, params: [solution_media], type: PollResults}
  ```
* Added

  ```
  traverseIncomingConstructor{predicate: updateMessagePoll, params: [poll, results], push_sources: [fileSourceMessage (unless messages.getScheduledMessages, updateNewScheduledMessage)], type: Update}
  ```
* Added

  ```
  traverseMethodResult{name: messages.getUnreadPollVotes}
  ```

##### Changed traversers incoming

* Changed messageMediaPhoto traverseIncomingConstructor from

  ```
  traverseIncomingConstructor{predicate: messageMediaPhoto, params: [photo], type: MessageMedia}
  ```

  to

  ```
  traverseIncomingConstructor{predicate: messageMediaPhoto, params: [photo, video], type: MessageMedia}
  ```
* Changed storyItem traverseIncomingConstructor from

  ```
  traverseIncomingConstructor{predicate: storyItem, params: [media], push_sources: [fileSourceStory (needs stories.getPinnedStories), fileSourceStory (needs stories.getStoriesArchive), fileSourceStory (needs stories.getStoriesByID), fileSourceStory (needs stories.getAlbumStories), fileSourceStory (needs peerStories), fileSourceStory], type: StoryItem}
  ```

  to

  ```
  traverseIncomingConstructor{predicate: storyItem, params: [media, music], push_sources: [fileSourceStory (needs stories.getPinnedStories), fileSourceStory (needs stories.getStoriesArchive), fileSourceStory (needs stories.getStoriesByID), fileSourceStory (needs stories.getAlbumStories), fileSourceStory (needs peerStories), fileSourceStory], type: StoryItem}
  ```

##### New traversers outgoing

* Added

  ```
  traverseMethodCall{name: messages.addPollAnswer, params: [answer]}
  ```
* Added

  ```
  traverseMethodCall{name: messages.reportMusicListen, params: [id]}
  ```
* Added

  ```
  traverseOutgoingConstructor{predicate: inputMediaPoll, params: [attached_media, solution_media], type: InputMedia}
  ```
* Added

  ```
  traverseOutgoingConstructor{predicate: inputPollAnswer, params: [media], type: PollAnswer}
  ```

##### Changed traversers outgoing

* Changed stories.editStory traverseMethodCall from

  ```
  traverseMethodCall{name: stories.editStory, params: [media]}
  ```

  to

  ```
  traverseMethodCall{name: stories.editStory, params: [media, music]}
  ```
* Changed stories.sendStory traverseMethodCall from

  ```
  traverseMethodCall{name: stories.sendStory, params: [media]}
  ```

  to

  ```
  traverseMethodCall{name: stories.sendStory, params: [media, music]}
  ```
* Changed inputMediaPhoto traverseOutgoingConstructor from

  ```
  traverseOutgoingConstructor{predicate: inputMediaPhoto, params: [id], type: InputMedia}
  ```

  to

  ```
  traverseOutgoingConstructor{predicate: inputMediaPhoto, params: [id, video], type: InputMedia}
  ```
* Changed inputMediaUploadedPhoto traverseOutgoingConstructor from

  ```
  traverseOutgoingConstructor{predicate: inputMediaUploadedPhoto, params: [file, stickers], type: InputMedia}
  ```

  to

  ```
  traverseOutgoingConstructor{predicate: inputMediaUploadedPhoto, params: [file, stickers, video], type: InputMedia}
  ```

### [Layer 223](https://core.telegram.org/schema?layer=223)

This layer introduces:

* [Login with Telegram via OAuth »](https://core.telegram.org/api/url-authorization#oauth-authorization)
* [Content protection for private chats »](https://core.telegram.org/api/content-protection#for-users)
* [Group member tags for all users, not just admins »](https://core.telegram.org/api/rank)
* [Date styled text entities »](https://core.telegram.org/api/entities#date-entities)
* [A new flow to obtain info about the admin that will become the owner of a basic group/supergroup/channel if the original owner leaves »](https://core.telegram.org/api/channel#leaving-groups-channels)
* [Ownership transfer for basic groups (along with supergroups and channels) »](https://core.telegram.org/api/channel#transferring-ownership-of-a-group-channel)
* [A new filter to return only resale gifts that can be bought using stars](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.getResaleStarGifts)

#### API constants

##### Incoming web events

Incoming web events documented [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/web-events#event-types).

* Added [`oauth_request` »](https://core.telegram.org/api/web-events#oauth-request)

##### Mini app outgoing events

Outgoing mini app events documented [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/webapps#incoming-events-client-to-mini-app).

* Added [`oauth_supported` »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/webapps#oauth-supported)
* Added [`oauth_result_confirmed` »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/webapps#oauth-result-confirmed)
* Added [`oauth_result_failed` »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/webapps#oauth-result-failed)

##### Premium features

[Telegram Premium feature identifiers »](https://core.telegram.org/api/premium#telegram-premium-features).

* Added [`pm_noforwards` »](https://core.telegram.org/api/premium#pm-noforwards)

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [messages.editChatCreator](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.editChatCreator) - Transfer the ownership of a basic group, supergroup or channel to another user, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/channel#transferring-ownership-of-a-group-channel) for the full flow.
* Added [messages.getFutureChatCreatorAfterLeave](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.getFutureChatCreatorAfterLeave) - Group/channel owners only: returns the ID of the user that will become the new owner of the group if we decide to leave the group, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/channel#leaving-groups-channels) for more info on the full flow.
* Added [messages.editChatParticipantRank](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.editChatParticipantRank) - Edit a group participant's [tag »](https://core.telegram.org/api/rank).
* Added [messages.declineUrlAuth](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.declineUrlAuth) - Decline an incoming [OAuth authorization request »](https://core.telegram.org/api/url-authorization#oauth-authorization), notifying the server that the user refused the login request.
* Added [messages.checkUrlAuthMatchCode](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.checkUrlAuthMatchCode) - Validate the match code selected by the user against the code shown on the login page, as part of the [OAuth authorization flow »](https://core.telegram.org/api/url-authorization#oauth-authorization).

##### Changed Methods

* Added **flags** parameter, changed type of **rank** from **string** to **flags.0?string** in [channels.editAdmin](https://core.telegram.org/method/channels.editAdmin)
* Added **in_app_origin** parameter in [messages.requestUrlAuth](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.requestUrlAuth)
* Added **share_phone_number**, **match_code** parameters in [messages.acceptUrlAuth](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.acceptUrlAuth)
* Added **flags**, **request_msg_id** parameters in [messages.toggleNoForwards](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.toggleNoForwards)
* Added **stars_only** parameter in [payments.getResaleStarGifts](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.getResaleStarGifts)

##### Deleted Methods

* Removed channels.editCreator
* Removed channels.getFutureCreatorAfterLeave

##### New Constructors

* Added [messageEntityFormattedDate](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageEntityFormattedDate) - Represents a specific point in time, rendered as specified [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/entities#date-entities)
* Added [updateChatParticipantRank](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updateChatParticipantRank) - The [tag »](https://core.telegram.org/api/rank) of a participant of a [basic group »](https://core.telegram.org/api/channel#basic-groups) has changed.
* Added [messageActionNoForwardsToggle](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionNoForwardsToggle) - Emitted only in private chats when enabling or disabling [content protection »](https://core.telegram.org/api/content-protection#for-users).
* Added [messageActionNoForwardsRequest](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionNoForwardsRequest) - Emitted only in private chats if the other side requested to disable [content protection »](https://core.telegram.org/api/content-protection#for-users).
* Added [channelAdminLogEventActionParticipantEditRank](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/channelAdminLogEventActionParticipantEditRank) - A participant's [tag »](https://core.telegram.org/api/rank) was changed.

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **flags**, **rank** parameters in [chatParticipant](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/chatParticipant)
* Added **from_rank** parameter in [message](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/message)
* Added **noforwards_my_enabled**, **noforwards_peer_enabled** parameters in [userFull](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/userFull)
* Added **rank** parameter in [channelParticipant](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/channelParticipant)
* Added **rank** parameter in [channelParticipantSelf](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/channelParticipantSelf)
* Added **flags**, **rank** parameters in [chatParticipantCreator](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/chatParticipantCreator)
* Added **flags**, **rank** parameters in [chatParticipantAdmin](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/chatParticipantAdmin)
* Added **rank** parameter in [channelParticipantBanned](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/channelParticipantBanned)
* Added **edit_rank** parameter in [channelAdminLogEventsFilter](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/channelAdminLogEventsFilter)
* Added **manage_ranks** parameter in [chatAdminRights](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/chatAdminRights)
* Added **edit_rank** parameter in [chatBannedRights](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/chatBannedRights)
* Added **match_codes_first**, **match_codes**, **user_id_hint** parameters in [urlAuthResultRequest](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/urlAuthResultRequest)

#### PUSH notification changes

##### New PUSH notifications

* Added [OAUTH_REQUEST](https://core.telegram.org/api/push-updates#oauth-request) - `Login request for {1} from {2}`

### [Layer 222](https://core.telegram.org/schema?layer=222)

This layer introduces the following features:

* [Custom keyboard button backgrounds and custom emoji button labels »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/buttons#button-styles)
* [AI message summarizer »](https://core.telegram.org/api/ai#summarize-messages)
* [Gift crafting »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#crafting-collectible-gifts)
* [A new flow to obtain info about the admin that will become the owner of a supergroup/channel if the original owner leaves »](https://core.telegram.org/api/channel#leaving-groups-channels)

#### API constants

##### Post story content types

Story content types accepted by [post story deep links »](https://core.telegram.org/api/links#post-story-links).

* Added `photo`
* Added `video`
* Added `live`

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [messages.getEmojiGameInfo](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.getEmojiGameInfo) - Fetch dice game information.
* Added [messages.summarizeText](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.summarizeText) - Summarize the contents of a message with AI, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/ai#summarize-messages) for more info.
* Added [payments.getCraftStarGifts](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.getCraftStarGifts) - Obtain owned [collectible gifts »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gifts) of a specific type that can be used for [crafting »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#crafting-collectible-gifts).
* Added [payments.craftStarGift](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.craftStarGift) - Craft a new [collectible gift »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gifts) by combining 1 to 4 owned collectible gifts of the same base gift type.
* Added [channels.getFutureCreatorAfterLeave](https://core.telegram.org/method/channels.getFutureCreatorAfterLeave)

##### Changed Methods

* Added **for_craft** parameter in [payments.getResaleStarGifts](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.getResaleStarGifts)

##### New Constructors

* Added [messages.emojiGameOutcome](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messages.emojiGameOutcome) - Dice game outcome.
* Added [inputMediaStakeDice](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputMediaStakeDice) - Dice game media.
* Added [messages.emojiGameUnavailable](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messages.emojiGameUnavailable) - Dice game information.
* Added [messages.emojiGameDiceInfo](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messages.emojiGameDiceInfo) - Dice game information.
* Added [updateEmojiGameInfo](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updateEmojiGameInfo) - Dice game update.
* Added [inputPasskeyCredentialFirebasePNV](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputPasskeyCredentialFirebasePNV) - Alternative [passkey](https://core.telegram.org/api/passkeys) credential that proves ownership of the account's phone number through a Firebase Phone Number Verification (PNV) token, used on official apps where a full WebAuthn [public-key passkey](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputPasskeyCredentialPublicKey) cannot be created, in a way similar to [Firebase SMS authentication »](https://core.telegram.org/api/auth).
* Added [starGiftAttributeRarity](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftAttributeRarity) - Exact rarity value for a collectible gift attribute.
* Added [starGiftAttributeRarityUncommon](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftAttributeRarityUncommon) - Represents uncommon collectible gift attribute rarity.
* Added [starGiftAttributeRarityRare](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftAttributeRarityRare) - Represents rare collectible gift attribute rarity.
* Added [starGiftAttributeRarityEpic](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftAttributeRarityEpic) - Represents epic collectible gift attribute rarity.
* Added [starGiftAttributeRarityLegendary](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftAttributeRarityLegendary) - Represents legendary collectible gift attribute rarity.
* Added [updateStarGiftCraftFail](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updateStarGiftCraftFail) - Indicates that a [crafting »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#crafting-collectible-gifts) attempt did not produce a new collectible gift.
* Added [keyboardButtonStyle](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/keyboardButtonStyle) - This constructor allows setting a custom background color and custom emoji label for a button, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/buttons#button-styles) for more info.
* Added [messageActionNewCreatorPending](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionNewCreatorPending) - Service message: emitted to a supergroup when the [group/channel creator leaves the group »](https://core.telegram.org/api/channel#leaving-groups-channels), indicating that ownership transfer is pending. The `new_creator_id` user will become the new owner after 7 days if the old owner does not rejoin.
* Added [messageActionChangeCreator](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionChangeCreator) - Service message: emitted to a supergroup when [ownership transfer completes after the old owner left the group »](https://core.telegram.org/api/channel#leaving-groups-channels) (7 days after the old owner left without rejoining), indicating that ownership has been transferred to a new owner.

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **summary_from_language** parameter in [message](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/message)
* Removed **legacy_ios** parameter in [messageService](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageService)
* Added **bot_forum_can_manage_topics** parameter in [user](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/user)
* Added **flags**, **style** parameters in [keyboardButton](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/keyboardButton)

* Added **flags**, **style** parameters in [keyboardButtonUrl](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/keyboardButtonUrl)

* Added **flags**, **style** parameters in [keyboardButtonRequestGeoLocation](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/keyboardButtonRequestGeoLocation)

* Added **flags**, **style** parameters in [keyboardButtonGame](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/keyboardButtonGame)
* Added **flags**, **style** parameters in [keyboardButtonBuy](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/keyboardButtonBuy)
* Added **style** parameter in [keyboardButtonUrlAuth](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/keyboardButtonUrlAuth)
* Added **style** parameter in [inputKeyboardButtonUrlAuth](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputKeyboardButtonUrlAuth)
* Added **request_phone_number**, **browser**, **platform**, **ip**, **region** parameters in [urlAuthResultRequest](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/urlAuthResultRequest)
* Added **flags** parameter, changed type of **url** from **string** to **flags.0?string** in [urlAuthResultAccepted](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/urlAuthResultAccepted)

* Added **flags**, **style** parameters in [keyboardButtonCopy](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/keyboardButtonCopy)
* Added **flags**, **crafted**, **rarity** parameters, removed **rarity_permille** parameter in [starGiftAttributeModel](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftAttributeModel)
* Added **rarity** parameter, removed **rarity_permille** parameter in [starGiftAttributePattern](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftAttributePattern)
* Added **rarity** parameter, removed **rarity_permille** parameter in [starGiftAttributeBackdrop](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftAttributeBackdrop)
* Added **burned**, **crafted**, **craft_chance_permille** parameters in [starGiftUnique](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftUnique)
* Added **craft**, **can_craft_at** parameters in [messageActionStarGiftUnique](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionStarGiftUnique)
* Added **can_craft_at** parameter in [savedStarGift](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/savedStarGift)

### [Layer 220](https://core.telegram.org/schema?layer=220)

This layer introduces the following features:

* [Added a new suggestion to setup a passkey »](https://core.telegram.org/api/config#setup-passkey)
* Added a new premium feature identifier for [paid private messages »](https://core.telegram.org/api/paid-messages)
* Some improvements for [auctions »](https://core.telegram.org/api/auctions):
  + Added a detailed description of all auction rounds as [StarGiftAuctionRound](https://core.telegram.org/type/StarGiftAuctionRound)s
  + Added `last_gift_num` to [allow computing the user's approximated position »](https://core.telegram.org/api/auctions#auction-flow)
  + Added fields to lookup auction gifts currently on resale, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/auctions) for more info.
* Added [payments.getStarGiftUpgradeAttributes](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.getStarGiftUpgradeAttributes) to obtain the **full** list of just the collectible attributes that may appear for a gift type once it's upgraded to a [collectible gift »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gifts).
* Moved some auction-related fields to [starGift](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGift)
* Added a new [starGiftBackground](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftBackground) constructor to represent the background palette of gift cards and auctions
* [Collectible gift purchase offers »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gift-purchase-offers)

#### API constants

##### Premium features

[Telegram Premium feature identifiers »](https://core.telegram.org/api/premium#telegram-premium-features).

* Added [`paid_messages` »](https://core.telegram.org/api/premium#paid-messages)

##### Suggested actions

[Basic suggestion identifiers »](https://core.telegram.org/api/config#basic-suggestions)

* Added [`SETUP_PASSKEY` »](https://core.telegram.org/api/config#setup-passkey)

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [payments.resolveStarGiftOffer](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.resolveStarGiftOffer) - Accept or decline a previously received [collectible gift purchase offer »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gift-purchase-offers), see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gift-purchase-offers) for the full flow.
* Added [payments.sendStarGiftOffer](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.sendStarGiftOffer) - Send an offer to purchase a [collectible gift »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gift-purchase-offers), see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gift-purchase-offers) for the full flow.
* Added [payments.getStarGiftUpgradeAttributes](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.getStarGiftUpgradeAttributes) - Obtains the **full** list of just the collectible attributes that may appear for a gift type once it's upgraded to a [collectible gift »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gifts).

##### New Constructors

* Added [messageActionStarGiftPurchaseOffer](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionStarGiftPurchaseOffer) - Contains an offer to purchase a [collectible gift »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gift-purchase-offers), see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gift-purchase-offers) for the full flow.
* Added [messageActionStarGiftPurchaseOfferDeclined](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionStarGiftPurchaseOfferDeclined) - A [collectible gift purchase offer »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gift-purchase-offers) was declined, or the offer expired, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gift-purchase-offers) for the full flow.
* Added [starGiftBackground](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftBackground) - Contains the default background palette of a [gift type »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#listing-all-possible-collectible-variants).
* Added [starGiftAuctionRound](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftAuctionRound) - Describes one or more [collectible gift auction rounds »](https://core.telegram.org/api/auctions).
* Added [starGiftAuctionRoundExtendable](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftAuctionRoundExtendable) - Describes one or more extendable [collectible gift auction rounds »](https://core.telegram.org/api/auctions).
* Added [payments.starGiftUpgradeAttributes](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/payments.starGiftUpgradeAttributes) - List of just the collectible attributes that may appear for a gift type once it's upgraded to a [collectible gift »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gifts).

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **legacy_ios** parameter in [messageService](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageService)
* Added **auction_start_date**, **upgrade_variants**, **background** parameters in [starGift](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGift)
* Added **gift_num** parameter in [messageActionStarGift](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionStarGift)
* Added **value_usd_amount**, **offer_min_stars** parameters in [starGiftUnique](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftUnique)
* Added **from_offer** parameter in [messageActionStarGiftUnique](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionStarGiftUnique)
* Added **gift_num** parameter in [savedStarGift](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/savedStarGift)
* Added **last_gift_num**, **rounds** parameters in [starGiftAuctionState](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftAuctionState)
* Added **flags**, **listed_count**, **fragment_listed_count**, **fragment_listed_url** parameters in [starGiftAuctionStateFinished](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftAuctionStateFinished)
* Added **chats** parameter in [payments.starGiftAuctionState](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/payments.starGiftAuctionState)
* Added **gift_num** parameter in [starGiftAuctionAcquiredGift](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftAuctionAcquiredGift)
* Added **chats** parameter in [payments.starGiftActiveAuctions](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/payments.starGiftActiveAuctions)
* Removed **center_color**, **edge_color**, **text_color** parameters in [webPageAttributeStarGiftAuction](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/webPageAttributeStarGiftAuction)

#### [Changes in the file reference database map file »](https://core.telegram.org/api/file-references)

##### New skipped incoming sources

* Added [payments.starGiftUpgradeAttributes](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/payments.starGiftUpgradeAttributes) - Contexts for star gifts are not yet implemented

##### New traversers incoming

* Added

  ```
  traverseIncomingConstructor{predicate: messageActionStarGiftPurchaseOffer, params: [gift], type: MessageAction}
  ```
* Added

  ```
  traverseIncomingConstructor{predicate: messageActionStarGiftPurchaseOfferDeclined, params: [gift], type: MessageAction}
  ```
* Added

  ```
  traverseIncomingConstructor{predicate: payments.starGiftUpgradeAttributes, params: [attributes], type: payments.StarGiftUpgradeAttributes}
  ```
* Added

  ```
  traverseMethodResult{name: payments.getStarGiftUpgradeAttributes}
  ```

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema)


### [Layer 219](https://core.telegram.org/schema?layer=219)

This layer introduces the following features:

* [Passkeys »](https://core.telegram.org/api/passkeys)
* [Message effects »](https://core.telegram.org/api/effects) support for [messages.forwardMessages](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.forwardMessages).

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [account.initPasskeyRegistration](https://core.telegram.org/method/account.initPasskeyRegistration) - Initialize passkey registration for the current account, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/passkeys#creating-a-passkey) for more info on the full flow.
* Added [account.registerPasskey](https://core.telegram.org/method/account.registerPasskey) - Complete passkey registration for the current account, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/passkeys#creating-a-passkey) for more info on the full flow.
* Added [account.getPasskeys](https://core.telegram.org/method/account.getPasskeys) - List the passkeys associated to the current account that can be used to log in, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/passkeys#list-passkeys) for more info on passkeys.
* Added [account.deletePasskey](https://core.telegram.org/method/account.deletePasskey) - Delete a passkey associated to the current account, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/passkeys#delete-passkeys) for more info.
* Added [auth.initPasskeyLogin](https://core.telegram.org/method/auth.initPasskeyLogin) - Initialize login with a passkey over an unauthenticated connection, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/passkeys#logging-in-with-a-passkey) for more info.
* Added [auth.finishPasskeyLogin](https://core.telegram.org/method/auth.finishPasskeyLogin) - Complete login with a passkey over an unauthenticated connection, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/passkeys#logging-in-with-a-passkey) for more info.

##### Changed Methods

* Added **effect** parameter in [messages.forwardMessages](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.forwardMessages)

##### New Constructors

* Added [passkey](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/passkey) - Human-readable info about a passkey associated to an account, returned when [creating a passkey »](https://core.telegram.org/api/passkeys#creating-a-passkey) or [listing passkeys »](https://core.telegram.org/api/passkeys#list-passkeys).
* Added [account.passkeys](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/account.passkeys) - List of [passkeys »](https://core.telegram.org/api/passkeys#list-passkeys) associated to the current account.
* Added [account.passkeyRegistrationOptions](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/account.passkeyRegistrationOptions) - Passkey registration options, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/passkeys#creating-a-passkey) for more info on the full flow.
* Added [auth.passkeyLoginOptions](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/auth.passkeyLoginOptions) - Passkey login options, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/passkeys#logging-in-with-a-passkey) for more info on the full flow.
* Added [inputPasskeyResponseRegister](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputPasskeyResponseRegister) - WebAuthn registration response used when registering a new passkey, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/passkeys#creating-a-passkey) for more info on the full flow.
* Added [inputPasskeyResponseLogin](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputPasskeyResponseLogin) - WebAuthn authentication response used when logging in with a passkey, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/passkeys#logging-in-with-a-passkey) for more info on the full flow.
* Added [inputPasskeyCredentialPublicKey](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputPasskeyCredentialPublicKey) - Public-key passkey credential used both for registration and for login, see [creating a passkey »](https://core.telegram.org/api/passkeys#creating-a-passkey) and [logging in with a passkey »](https://core.telegram.org/api/passkeys#logging-in-with-a-passkey) for the full flows.

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **offer** parameter in [starsTransaction](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starsTransaction)

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema)

```
starsTransaction#13659eb0 flags:# refund:flags.3?true pending:flags.4?true failed:flags.6?true gift:flags.10?true reaction:flags.11?true stargift_upgrade:flags.18?true business_transfer:flags.21?true stargift_resale:flags.22?true posts_search:flags.24?true stargift_prepaid_upgrade:flags.25?true stargift_drop_original_details:flags.26?true phonegroup_message:flags.27?true stargift_auction_bid:flags.28?true offer:flags.29?true id:string amount:StarsAmount date:int peer:StarsTransactionPeer title:flags.0?string description:flags.1?string photo:flags.2?WebDocument transaction_date:flags.5?int transaction_url:flags.5?string bot_payload:flags.7?bytes msg_id:flags.8?int extended_media:flags.9?Vector<MessageMedia> subscription_period:flags.12?int giveaway_post_id:flags.13?int stargift:flags.14?StarGift floodskip_number:flags.15?int starref_commission_permille:flags.16?int starref_peer:flags.17?Peer starref_amount:flags.17?StarsAmount paid_messages:flags.19?int premium_gift_months:flags.20?int ads_proceeds_from_date:flags.23?int ads_proceeds_to_date:flags.23?int = StarsTransaction;  

passkey#98613ebf flags:# id:string name:string date:int software_emoji_id:flags.0?long last_usage_date:flags.1?int = Passkey;  

account.passkeys#f8e0aa1c passkeys:Vector<Passkey> = account.Passkeys;
account.passkeyRegistrationOptions#e16b5ce1 options:DataJSON = account.PasskeyRegistrationOptions;
auth.passkeyLoginOptions#e2037789 options:DataJSON = auth.PasskeyLoginOptions;  

inputPasskeyResponseRegister#3e63935c client_data:DataJSON attestation_data:bytes = InputPasskeyResponse;
inputPasskeyResponseLogin#c31fc14a client_data:DataJSON authenticator_data:bytes signature:bytes user_handle:string = InputPasskeyResponse;
inputPasskeyCredentialPublicKey#3c27b78f id:string raw_id:string response:InputPasskeyResponse = InputPasskeyCredential;  

---functions---  

messages.forwardMessages#13704a7c flags:# silent:flags.5?true background:flags.6?true with_my_score:flags.8?true drop_author:flags.11?true drop_media_captions:flags.12?true noforwards:flags.14?true allow_paid_floodskip:flags.19?true from_peer:InputPeer id:Vector<int> random_id:Vector<long> to_peer:InputPeer top_msg_id:flags.9?int reply_to:flags.22?InputReplyTo schedule_date:flags.10?int schedule_repeat_period:flags.24?int send_as:flags.13?InputPeer quick_reply_shortcut:flags.17?InputQuickReplyShortcut effect:flags.18?long video_timestamp:flags.20?int allow_paid_stars:flags.21?long suggested_post:flags.23?SuggestedPost = Updates;  

account.initPasskeyRegistration#429547e8 = account.PasskeyRegistrationOptions;
account.registerPasskey#55b41fd6 credential:InputPasskeyCredential = Passkey;
account.getPasskeys#ea1f0c52 = account.Passkeys;
account.deletePasskey#f5b5563f id:string = Bool;
auth.initPasskeyLogin#518ad0b7 api_id:int api_hash:string = auth.PasskeyLoginOptions;
auth.finishPasskeyLogin#9857ad07 flags:# credential:InputPasskeyCredential from_dc_id:flags.0?int from_auth_key_id:flags.0?long = auth.Authorization;
```

### [Layer 218](https://core.telegram.org/schema?layer=218)

This layer introduces the following features:

* [Collectible gift auctions »](https://core.telegram.org/api/auctions)

#### API constants

##### Web page type values

Web page type values from the [webPage](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/webPage) constructor.

* Added `telegram_auction`

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [payments.getStarGiftAuctionState](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.getStarGiftAuctionState) - Returns info about a [collectible gift auction »](https://core.telegram.org/api/auctions); also subscribes the user to auction updates, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/auctions) for more info on the full flow.
* Added [payments.getStarGiftAuctionAcquiredGifts](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.getStarGiftAuctionAcquiredGifts) - Fetches all the gifts that the current user won in an [auction](https://core.telegram.org/api/auctions).
* Added [payments.getStarGiftActiveAuctions](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.getStarGiftActiveAuctions) - Fetches all currently active [gift auctions](https://core.telegram.org/api/auctions) **the user has ever bid on** (including auctions where the user was outbid and their bid was returned), as long as the auction hasn't ended yet.

##### New Constructors

* Added [inputInvoiceStarGiftAuctionBid](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputInvoiceStarGiftAuctionBid) - Used to place a bid in a [collectible gift auction »](https://core.telegram.org/api/auctions).
* Added [auctionBidLevel](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/auctionBidLevel) - Describes a bid in an [auction](https://core.telegram.org/api/auctions).
* Added [starGiftAuctionStateNotModified](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftAuctionStateNotModified) - Returned only by auction methods (never by updates) if the passed `version` is equal to the remote [auction »](https://core.telegram.org/api/auctions).`version`, meaning auction information hasn't changed over the locally cached version.
* Added [starGiftAuctionState](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftAuctionState) - Represents an active or pending [auction »](https://core.telegram.org/api/auctions).
* Added [starGiftAuctionStateFinished](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftAuctionStateFinished) - Represents a finished [auction »](https://core.telegram.org/api/auctions).
* Added [starGiftAuctionUserState](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftAuctionUserState) - Contains information about the current user's state in an [auction »](https://core.telegram.org/api/auctions).
* Added [updateStarGiftAuctionState](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updateStarGiftAuctionState) - Contains updates to [auction state, see here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/auctions) for more info on how to enable these updates.
* Added [updateStarGiftAuctionUserState](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updateStarGiftAuctionUserState) - Contains updates to [auction state related to the current user, see here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/auctions) for more info on how to enable these updates.
* Added [payments.starGiftAuctionState](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/payments.starGiftAuctionState) - Describes a [collectible gift auction »](https://core.telegram.org/api/auctions).
* Added [starGiftAuctionAcquiredGift](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftAuctionAcquiredGift) - Describes a gift that the current user won in an auction.
* Added [payments.starGiftAuctionAcquiredGifts](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/payments.starGiftAuctionAcquiredGifts) - Describes all the gifts that the current user won in an [auction](https://core.telegram.org/api/auctions).
* Added [starGiftActiveAuctionState](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftActiveAuctionState) - Contains info about [an auction where the user has placed a bid »](https://core.telegram.org/api/auctions).
* Added [payments.starGiftActiveAuctionsNotModified](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/payments.starGiftActiveAuctionsNotModified) - The list of currently active [gift auctions](https://core.telegram.org/api/auctions) **where the user has placed a bid** hasn't changed.
* Added [payments.starGiftActiveAuctions](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/payments.starGiftActiveAuctions) - Describes all currently active [gift auctions](https://core.telegram.org/api/auctions) **where the user has placed a bid**.
* Added [inputStarGiftAuction](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputStarGiftAuction) - Used to fetch [auctions](https://core.telegram.org/api/auctions) using the ID of the linked [collectible gift](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gifts).
* Added [inputStarGiftAuctionSlug](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputStarGiftAuctionSlug) - Used to fetch [auctions](https://core.telegram.org/api/auctions) using the [slug of an auction deep link »](https://core.telegram.org/api/links#auction-links).
* Added [webPageAttributeStarGiftAuction](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/webPageAttributeStarGiftAuction) - Contains info about a [collectible gift auction »](https://core.telegram.org/api/auctions) for a [webPage](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/webPage) preview of an [auction »](https://core.telegram.org/api/auctions) (the [webPage](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/webPage) will have a `type` of `telegram_auction`).

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **stargift_auction_bid** parameter in [starsTransaction](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starsTransaction)
* Added **auction**, **auction_slug**, **gifts_per_round** parameters in [starGift](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGift)
* Added **auction_acquired**, **to_id** parameters in [messageActionStarGift](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionStarGift)

#### [Changes in the file reference database map file »](https://core.telegram.org/api/file-references)

##### New traversers incoming

* Added

  ```
  traverseIncomingConstructor{predicate: payments.starGiftActiveAuctions, params: [auctions], type: payments.StarGiftActiveAuctions}
  ```
* Added

  ```
  traverseIncomingConstructor{predicate: payments.starGiftAuctionState, params: [gift], type: payments.StarGiftAuctionState}
  ```
* Added

  ```
  traverseIncomingConstructor{predicate: starGiftActiveAuctionState, params: [gift], type: StarGiftActiveAuctionState}
  ```
* Added

  ```
  traverseIncomingConstructor{predicate: webPageAttributeStarGiftAuction, params: [gift], type: WebPageAttribute}
  ```
* Added

  ```
  traverseMethodResult{name: payments.getStarGiftActiveAuctions}
  ```
* Added

  ```
  traverseMethodResult{name: payments.getStarGiftAuctionState}
  ```

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema)

