### [Layer 217](https://core.telegram.org/schema?layer=217)

This layer introduces:

* [Live stories »](https://core.telegram.org/api/group-calls#live-stories)
* [Recent story summaries »](https://core.telegram.org/api/stories#recent-story-summaries)
* [Methods to delete group call messages and live story comments »](https://core.telegram.org/api/group-calls#in-call-messages)
* [Repeating scheduled messages »](https://core.telegram.org/api/scheduled-messages#repeating-scheduled-messages)
* [Privacy settings for the music tab on profiles »](https://core.telegram.org/api/profile#music)
* [Anonymous group admins »](https://core.telegram.org/api/rights#anonymous-admins) can now [toggle items in checklists »](https://core.telegram.org/api/todo#mark-items-as-completed)
* [Suggestions to setup a login email »](https://core.telegram.org/api/auth#email-verification)

#### API constants

##### Suggested actions

[Basic suggestion identifiers »](https://core.telegram.org/api/config#basic-suggestions)

* Added [`SETUP_LOGIN_EMAIL` »](https://core.telegram.org/api/config#setup-login-email)
* Added [`SETUP_LOGIN_EMAIL_NOSKIP` »](https://core.telegram.org/api/config#setup-login-email-noskip)

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [stories.startLive](https://core.telegram.org/method/stories.startLive) - Start a live story, optionally using RTMP livestream mode, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/group-calls#live-stories) for the full flow.
* Added [phone.deleteGroupCallMessages](https://core.telegram.org/method/phone.deleteGroupCallMessages) - Delete specific messages from the [in-call message overlay »](https://core.telegram.org/api/group-calls#in-call-messages) of a video chat/livestream or live story, including in RTMP mode.
* Added [phone.deleteGroupCallParticipantMessages](https://core.telegram.org/method/phone.deleteGroupCallParticipantMessages) - As an admin, delete all messages from a specific participant in the [in-call message overlay »](https://core.telegram.org/api/group-calls#in-call-messages) of a video chat/livestream or live story, including in RTMP mode.
* Added [phone.getGroupCallStars](https://core.telegram.org/method/phone.getGroupCallStars) - Fetch a live story's total donations and top donors, see [paid live story donations »](https://core.telegram.org/api/group-calls#paid-live-story-donations).
* Added [phone.saveDefaultSendAs](https://core.telegram.org/method/phone.saveDefaultSendAs) - Save the default peer displayed as the author of live story comments and reactions, see [in-call messages »](https://core.telegram.org/api/group-calls#in-call-messages).

##### Changed Methods

* Added **schedule_repeat_period** parameter in [messages.sendMessage](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.sendMessage)
* Added **schedule_repeat_period** parameter in [messages.sendMedia](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.sendMedia)
* Added **schedule_repeat_period** parameter in [messages.forwardMessages](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.forwardMessages)
* Added **schedule_repeat_period** parameter in [messages.editMessage](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.editMessage)
* Added **send_paid_messages_stars** parameter in [phone.toggleGroupCallSettings](https://core.telegram.org/method/phone.toggleGroupCallSettings)
* Added **for_live_stories** parameter in [channels.getSendAs](https://core.telegram.org/method/channels.getSendAs)
* Added **flags**, **live_story** parameters in [phone.getGroupCallStreamRtmpUrl](https://core.telegram.org/method/phone.getGroupCallStreamRtmpUrl)
* Changed type of [stories.getPeerMaxIDs](https://core.telegram.org/method/stories.getPeerMaxIDs) from **Vector<int>** to **Vector<RecentStory>**
* Changed type of [phone.sendGroupCallMessage](https://core.telegram.org/method/phone.sendGroupCallMessage) from **Bool** to **Updates**
* Added **flags**, **allow_paid_stars**, **send_as** parameters in [phone.sendGroupCallMessage](https://core.telegram.org/method/phone.sendGroupCallMessage)

##### New Constructors

* Added [messageMediaVideoStream](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageMediaVideoStream) - Identifies the active group call associated with a [live story »](https://core.telegram.org/api/group-calls#live-stories).
* Added [updateDeleteGroupCallMessages](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updateDeleteGroupCallMessages) - Indicates that messages were deleted from the [in-call message overlay »](https://core.telegram.org/api/group-calls#in-call-messages) of a video chat/livestream or live story, including in RTMP mode.
* Added [groupCallMessage](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/groupCallMessage) - Represents an [in-call message »](https://core.telegram.org/api/group-calls#in-call-messages), emoji reaction, paid live story comment or standalone paid live story donation.
* Added [inputPrivacyKeySavedMusic](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputPrivacyKeySavedMusic) - Whether the user can see the [songs we pinned to our profile »](https://core.telegram.org/api/profile#music).
* Added [privacyKeySavedMusic](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/privacyKeySavedMusic) - Whether the user can see the [songs we pinned to our profile »](https://core.telegram.org/api/profile#music).
* Added [groupCallDonor](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/groupCallDonor) - Describes a live story donor in the [donation leaderboard »](https://core.telegram.org/api/group-calls#paid-live-story-donations).
* Added [phone.groupCallStars](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/phone.groupCallStars) - Contains a live story's total donations and top donors, see [paid live story donations »](https://core.telegram.org/api/group-calls#paid-live-story-donations).
* Added [recentStory](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/recentStory) - Summary of a peer's [active stories »](https://core.telegram.org/api/stories#recent-story-summaries), embedded in [user](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/user).`stories_max_id` and [channel](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/channel).`stories_max_id` and returned by [stories.getPeerMaxIDs](https://core.telegram.org/method/stories.getPeerMaxIDs).

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **schedule_repeat_period** parameter in [message](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/message)
* Changed type of **stories_max_id** from **flags2.5?int** to **flags2.5?RecentStory** in [user](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/user)
* Changed type of **stories_max_id** from **flags2.4?int** to **flags2.4?RecentStory** in [channel](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/channel)
* Added **send_paid_messages_stars**, **default_send_as** parameters in [groupCall](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/groupCall)
* Added **paid_stars_total** parameter in [groupCallParticipant](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/groupCallParticipant)
* Added **live_story**, **peer** parameters, removed **chat_id** parameter in [updateGroupCall](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updateGroupCall)
* Added **days** parameter, removed **months** parameter in [messageActionGiftPremium](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionGiftPremium)
* Added **live** parameter in [storyItemSkipped](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/storyItemSkipped)
* Added **days** parameter, removed **months** parameter in [payments.checkedGiftCode](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/payments.checkedGiftCode)
* Added **days** parameter, removed **months** parameter in [messageActionGiftCode](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionGiftCode)
* Added **phonegroup_message** parameter in [starsTransaction](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starsTransaction)
* Added **disallow_stargifts_from_channels** parameter in [disallowedGiftsSettings](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/disallowedGiftsSettings)
* Changed type of **completed_by** from **long** to **Peer** in [todoCompletion](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/todoCompletion)
* Removed **from_id**, **random_id** parameters, changed type of **message** from **TextWithEntities** to **GroupCallMessage** in [updateGroupCallMessage](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updateGroupCallMessage)

### [Layer 216](https://core.telegram.org/schema?layer=216)

This layer introduces the following new features:

* [Bot forums »](https://core.telegram.org/api/forum#bot-forums)
* [Private contact notes »](https://core.telegram.org/api/contacts#private-notes-for-contacts)
* [Suggested birthdays »](https://core.telegram.org/api/profile#birthday)
* [Collectible gifts can now have associated message color palettes »](https://core.telegram.org/api/colors#collectible-message-palettes)
* [Live bot response streaming »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/ai#live-response-streaming).
* [Hosted collectible gifts: linking a collectible gift on the TON blockchain to a Telegram profile »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#hosted-collectible-gifts)

#### API constants

##### Client configs

[Client configuration keys »](https://core.telegram.org/api/config#client-configuration).

* Added [`contact_note_length_limit` »](https://core.telegram.org/api/config#contact-note-length-limit)
* Added [`group_call_message_length_limit` »](https://core.telegram.org/api/config#group-call-message-length-limit)
* Added [`group_call_message_ttl` »](https://core.telegram.org/api/config#group-call-message-ttl)
* Added [`message_typing_draft_ttl` »](https://core.telegram.org/api/config#message-typing-draft-ttl)

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [messages.getForumTopics](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.getForumTopics) - Get [topics of a forum](https://core.telegram.org/api/forum)
* Added [messages.getForumTopicsByID](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.getForumTopicsByID) - Get forum topics by their ID
* Added [messages.editForumTopic](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.editForumTopic) - Edit [forum topic](https://core.telegram.org/api/forum).
* Added [messages.updatePinnedForumTopic](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.updatePinnedForumTopic) - Pin or unpin [forum topics](https://core.telegram.org/api/forum)
* Added [messages.reorderPinnedForumTopics](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.reorderPinnedForumTopics) - Reorder pinned forum topics
* Added [messages.createForumTopic](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.createForumTopic) - Create a [forum topic](https://core.telegram.org/api/forum).
* Added [messages.deleteTopicHistory](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.deleteTopicHistory) - Delete message history of a [forum topic](https://core.telegram.org/api/forum)
* Added [phone.sendGroupCallMessage](https://core.telegram.org/method/phone.sendGroupCallMessage) - Send an in-call message to all participants of a video chat/livestream or live story, including in RTMP mode, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/group-calls#in-call-messages) for more info.
* Added [phone.sendGroupCallEncryptedMessage](https://core.telegram.org/method/phone.sendGroupCallEncryptedMessage) - Send an E2E-encrypted message or emoji reaction to all participants of a conference call. This method can only be used with conferences; see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/end-to-end/group-calls#conference-in-call-messages) for the serialization and encryption process.
* Added [contacts.updateContactNote](https://core.telegram.org/method/contacts.updateContactNote) - Update the private note associated to a contact; see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/contacts#private-notes-for-contacts) for more info.
* Added [users.suggestBirthday](https://core.telegram.org/method/users.suggestBirthday) - Suggest a birthday to another user, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/profile#birthday) for more info on birthdays in the API.

##### Changed Methods

* Added **note** parameter in [contacts.addContact](https://core.telegram.org/method/contacts.addContact)
* Added **messages_enabled** parameter in [phone.toggleGroupCallSettings](https://core.telegram.org/method/phone.toggleGroupCallSettings)
* Removed **background_emoji_id** parameter, changed type of **color** from **flags.2?int** to **flags.2?PeerColor** in [account.updateColor](https://core.telegram.org/method/account.updateColor)
* Added **peer_color_available**, **exclude_hosted** parameters in [payments.getSavedStarGifts](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.getSavedStarGifts)

##### Deleted Methods

* Removed channels.createForumTopic
* Removed channels.getForumTopics
* Removed channels.getForumTopicsByID
* Removed channels.editForumTopic
* Removed channels.updatePinnedForumTopic
* Removed channels.deleteTopicHistory
* Removed channels.reorderPinnedForumTopics

##### New Constructors

* Added [sendMessageTextDraftAction](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/sendMessageTextDraftAction) - Used by bots to implement [live message streaming »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/ai#live-response-streaming).
* Added [updateGroupCallMessage](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updateGroupCallMessage) - A new message, reaction, paid comment or donation was received through the [in-call message overlay »](https://core.telegram.org/api/group-calls#in-call-messages).
* Added [updateGroupCallEncryptedMessage](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updateGroupCallEncryptedMessage) - A new E2E-encrypted message or emoji reaction was received in a conference call, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/end-to-end/group-calls#receiving-and-decrypting-a-message) for the decryption process.
* Added [updatePinnedForumTopic](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updatePinnedForumTopic) - A [forum topic »](https://core.telegram.org/api/forum#forum-topics) was pinned or unpinned.
* Added [updatePinnedForumTopics](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updatePinnedForumTopics) - The [pinned topics](https://core.telegram.org/api/forum#forum-topics) of a forum have changed.
* Added [peerColorCollectible](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/peerColorCollectible) - Represents a [color palette »](https://core.telegram.org/api/colors) associated to a [collectible gift »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gifts), see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/colors#collectible-gift-palettes) for more info.
* Added [inputPeerColorCollectible](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputPeerColorCollectible) - Represents a [color palette »](https://core.telegram.org/api/colors) associated to a [collectible gift »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gifts), see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/colors#collectible-gift-palettes) for more info.
* Added [messageActionSuggestBirthday](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionSuggestBirthday) - A new birthday was suggested using [users.suggestBirthday](https://core.telegram.org/method/users.suggestBirthday), see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/profile#birthday) for more info on birthdays in the API.

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **flags**, **note** parameters in [inputPhoneContact](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputPhoneContact)
* Added **note** parameter in [userFull](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/userFull)
* Added **topics** parameter in [messages.messages](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messages.messages)
* Added **topics** parameter in [messages.messagesSlice](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messages.messagesSlice)
* Added **flags**, **top_msg_id** parameters in [updateUserTyping](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updateUserTyping)
* Added **top_msg_id** parameter in [updateReadHistoryInbox](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updateReadHistoryInbox)
* Added **bot_forum_view** parameter in [user](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/user)
* Added **messages_enabled**, **can_change_messages_enabled**, **min** parameters in [groupCall](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/groupCall)
* Added **title_missing**, **peer** parameters in [forumTopic](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/forumTopic)
* Added **title_missing** parameter in [messageActionTopicCreate](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionTopicCreate)
* Added **peer_color_available** parameter in [starGift](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGift)
* Added **peer_color**, **host_id** parameters in [starGiftUnique](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftUnique)
* Added **assigned** parameter in [messageActionStarGiftUnique](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionStarGiftUnique)

##### Deleted Constructors

* Removed updateChannelPinnedTopic
* Removed updateChannelPinnedTopics

#### PUSH notification changes

##### New PUSH notifications

* Added [MESSAGE_SUGGEST_BIRTHDAY](https://core.telegram.org/api/push-updates#message-suggest-birthday) - `{1} suggested you your birthday`

#### [Changes in the file reference database map file »](https://core.telegram.org/api/file-references)

##### New traversers incoming

* Added

  ```
  traverseMethodResult{name: messages.getForumTopics}
  ```
* Added

  ```
  traverseMethodResult{name: messages.getForumTopicsByID}
  ```

##### Deleted Traversers incoming

* Removed

  ```
  traverseMethodResult{name: channels.getForumTopics}
  ```
* Removed

  ```
  traverseMethodResult{name: channels.getForumTopicsByID}
  ```

#### [End-to-end schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema/end-to-end) changes

##### New Constructors

* Added [jsonObjectValue](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/jsonObjectValue) - JSON key: value pair
* Added [jsonNull](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/jsonNull) - null JSON value
* Added [jsonBool](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/jsonBool) - JSON boolean value
* Added [jsonNumber](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/jsonNumber) - JSON numeric value
* Added [jsonString](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/jsonString) - JSON string
* Added [jsonArray](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/jsonArray) - JSON array
* Added [jsonObject](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/jsonObject) - JSON object value
* Added [textWithEntities](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/textWithEntities) - Styled text with [message entities](https://core.telegram.org/api/entities)
* Added [groupCallMessage](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/groupCallMessage) - Represents an [in-call message »](https://core.telegram.org/api/group-calls#in-call-messages), emoji reaction, paid live story comment or standalone paid live story donation.

```

jsonObject#99c1d49d value:Vector<JSONObjectValue> = JSONValue;

groupCallMessage#907ce88e random_id:long message:TextWithEntities = GroupCallMessage;
```

### [Layer 215](https://core.telegram.org/schema?layer=215)

This layer introduces the following new features and improvements:

* [Allow removing the starGiftAttributeOriginalDetails from an owned gift »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#dropping-the-original-details-of-an-upgraded-gift).
* [payments.starGiftUpgradePreview](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/payments.starGiftUpgradePreview) now returns a list of future [gift upgrade](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gifts) prices in **prices** and **next_prices**.
* Improved pagination for [account.getUniqueGiftChatThemes](https://core.telegram.org/method/account.getUniqueGiftChatThemes), also added a note indicating that the method may return less than `limit` results, even if the actual number of remaining results is `>= limit`, pagination should be used to fetch them all.

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [auth.checkPaidAuth](https://core.telegram.org/method/auth.checkPaidAuth) - Checks the status of a [login payment](https://core.telegram.org/api/auth#paid-auth).

##### Changed Methods

* Changed type of **offset** from **int** to **string** in [account.getUniqueGiftChatThemes](https://core.telegram.org/method/account.getUniqueGiftChatThemes)

##### New Constructors

* Added [inputInvoicePremiumAuthCode](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputInvoicePremiumAuthCode) - Used to pay for login codes, in case of high cost of SMS verification codes for the user's country/provider, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/auth#paid-auth) for more info.
* Added [inputInvoiceStarGiftDropOriginalDetails](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputInvoiceStarGiftDropOriginalDetails) - Used to pay for for the removal of the [starGiftAttributeOriginalDetails](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftAttributeOriginalDetails) attribute from a collectible gift, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#dropping-the-original-details-of-an-upgraded-gift) for the full flow.
* Added [starGiftUpgradePrice](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftUpgradePrice) - Indicates the price for a [gift upgrade »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gifts) starting from a specific point in time.

##### Changed Constructors

* Changed type of **next_offset** from **flags.0?int** to **flags.0?string** in [account.chatThemes](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/account.chatThemes)
* Added **stargift_drop_original_details** parameter in [starsTransaction](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starsTransaction)
* Added **drop_original_details_stars** parameter in [messageActionStarGiftUnique](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionStarGiftUnique)
* Added **prices**, **next_prices** parameters in [payments.starGiftUpgradePreview](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/payments.starGiftUpgradePreview)
* Added **drop_original_details_stars** parameter in [savedStarGift](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/savedStarGift)
* Added **currency**, **amount** parameters in [auth.sentCodePaymentRequired](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/auth.sentCodePaymentRequired)

### [Layer 214](https://core.telegram.org/schema?layer=214)

To view all changes since the last documentation refresh, start reading at [layer 196](https://core.telegram.org/api/layers#layer-196). This documentation refresh *also* brings the following changes:

* [A brand new file reference database map file »](https://core.telegram.org/api/file-references): can be used to automatically codegen a fully functional file reference database, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/file-references) for more info.

  + The map file generator is open source, and is specifically designed to be easy to run on newer, even experimental layers, see [here for more info »](https://core.telegram.org/api/file-reference-generator).
  + Layer diffs will now list changes to the file reference map file as well.
* [The RPC error database was updated »](https://core.telegram.org/api/errors), and it now has the following extra fields:

  + `business_supported` - Contains the full list of methods that can be used by bots over a [business connection with invokeWithBusinessConnection](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/connected-business-bots).
  + `unauthed_allowed` - Contains the full list of methods that can be used by not yet logged in connections.
* All methods in the documentation now explicitly state if they can be used by bots, users or both, and if they can be used over a business connection or over an unauthorized connection.
* Updated the list of methods that can be used over a [business connection](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/connected-business-bots)

  + [stories.sendStory](https://core.telegram.org/method/stories.sendStory) and [stories.editStory](https://core.telegram.org/method/stories.editStory) can also be used to post and edit stories on behalf of a connected business account: in this case, simply pass the business account's peer in `peer`, *without* wrapping the request in an [invokeWithBusinessConnection »](https://core.telegram.org/method/invokeWithBusinessConnection) query.  
    Note that [stories.editStory](https://core.telegram.org/method/stories.editStory) can only be used to edit stories posted by the same business bot on behalf of the user.
* Updated the list of methods that can be used over an [unauthed connection](https://core.telegram.org/api/auth#we-are-authorized)
* The [documentation for the profile page was also reorganized, to include the full list of profile tabs to show, along with instructions on how to populate them »](https://core.telegram.org/api/profile).
* [Corrected hash generation instructions for messages.getScheduledHistory, the correct order is id, edit_date, date; not id, date, edit_date »](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.getScheduledHistory)
* Added documentation on [frozen accounts »](https://core.telegram.org/api/auth#frozen-accounts)
* [Added a new client configuration key for the maximum number of allowed quiz poll answers](https://core.telegram.org/api/config#poll-answers-max)
* [Added a new client configuration key for the maximum amount of Telegram Stars that can be withdrawn from a channel or bot's balance »](https://core.telegram.org/api/config#stars-revenue-withdrawal-max)
* [Added support for age verification, if required by the current country's legislation »](https://core.telegram.org/api/age-verification).
* Added device storage and secure storage APIs for [mini apps](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/webapps), using the following events:

  + [web_app_device_storage_save_key](https://core.telegram.org/api/web-events#web-app-device-storage-save-key) - Save or remove a value from the device's local storage associated with this user and mini app
  + [web_app_device_storage_get_key](https://core.telegram.org/api/web-events#web-app-device-storage-get-key) - Get a value from the device's local storage associated with this user and mini app
  + [web_app_device_storage_clear](https://core.telegram.org/api/web-events#web-app-device-storage-clear) - Clear the device's local storage associated with this user and mini app
  + [web_app_secure_storage_save_key](https://core.telegram.org/api/web-events#web-app-secure-storage-save-key) - Save or remove a value from the device's secure storage associated with this user and mini app
  + [web_app_secure_storage_get_key](https://core.telegram.org/api/web-events#web-app-secure-storage-get-key) - Get a value from the device's secure storage associated with this user and mini app
  + [web_app_secure_storage_restore_key](https://core.telegram.org/api/web-events#web-app-secure-storage-restore-key) - Restore a value to the device's secure storage associated with this user and mini app
  + [web_app_secure_storage_clear](https://core.telegram.org/api/web-events#web-app-secure-storage-clear) - Clear the device's secure storage associated with this user and mini app
* Add support for the new `manage_direct_messages` [admin right](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/chatAdminRights) in [group/channel bot links](https://core.telegram.org/api/links#group-channel-bot-links).
* Updated the documentation for bot API dialog IDs to include support for the new monoforum ID range, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/ids#monoforum-ids) for more info.

  + Specifically, the bot API dialog ID new ranges from `-4000000000000` to `1099511627775` (previously the range was `-2002147483648` to `1099511627775`).
  + The transformed range for bot API monoforum dialog IDs is `-2002147483649` to `-4000000000000` inclusively.
* Push notifications can now have a custom `report_delivery_until_date` parameter for [Telegram Gateway verification messages](https://telegram.org/blog/star-messages-gateway-2-0-and-more#save-even-more-on-user-verification): if set, the message is a Telegram Gateway verification code, and [messages.reportMessagesDelivery](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.reportMessagesDelivery) should be invoked with the `push` flag set.
* Added a correction to the [paid reactions documentation »](https://core.telegram.org/api/reactions#paid-reactions): the `random_id` argument of [messages.sendPaidReaction](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.sendPaidReaction) **must** be composed of a 64-bit integer where the lower 32 bits are random, and the higher 32 bits **are equal to the current unixtime**, i.e. `uint64_t random_id = (time() << 32) | ((uint64_t)random_uint32_t())`: this differs from the `random_id` format of all other methods in the API, which just take 64 random bits.
* Clarified that adding a new recent reaction should trigger modification of the cached list and regeneration of the hash with a custom algorithm, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/reactions#recent-reactions) for more info.
* Clarified that modifying saved message tags should trigger modification of the cached list and regeneration of the hash with a custom algorithm, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/saved-messages#tags) for more info.
* The [webpage type list »](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/webPage) was updated.
* [Clarified](https://core.telegram.org/api/invoking) that in some cases such as updates from big channels, the API may return constructors from older layers, different from the connection's current layer.  
  Clients should treat this as a `500` server error, and handle it by closing and reopening the TCP socket, re-reinitializing the session with [initConnection](https://core.telegram.org/method/initConnection) and invoking [getDifference](https://core.telegram.org/api/updates).
* A whole bunch of new features and articles, [start reading at layer 196](https://core.telegram.org/api/layers#layer-196) to view them all. And here are the changes in this layer:
* [Set a collectible gift as chat theme »](https://core.telegram.org/api/themes#chat-themes)
* Allow sending an email to Telegram's support team when getting [auth.sentCodePaymentRequired](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/auth.sentCodePaymentRequired) during login (official clients only).

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [account.getUniqueGiftChatThemes](https://core.telegram.org/method/account.getUniqueGiftChatThemes) - Obtain all [chat themes »](https://core.telegram.org/api/themes#chat-themes) associated to owned or [hosted collectible gifts »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#hosted-collectible-gifts).

##### Changed Methods

* Added **theme** parameter, removed **emoticon** parameter in [messages.setChatTheme](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.setChatTheme)

##### New Constructors

* Added [chatTheme](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/chatTheme) - A chat theme
* Added [chatThemeUniqueGift](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/chatThemeUniqueGift) - A chat theme based on a [collectible gift »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gifts).
* Added [account.chatThemesNotModified](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/account.chatThemesNotModified) - The available chat themes were not modified
* Added [account.chatThemes](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/account.chatThemes) - Available [chat themes](https://core.telegram.org/api/themes#chat-themes)
* Added [inputChatThemeEmpty](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputChatThemeEmpty) - Remove any currently configured theme.
* Added [inputChatTheme](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputChatTheme) - Set an emoji-based chat theme, returned by [account.getChatThemes](https://core.telegram.org/method/account.getChatThemes).
* Added [inputChatThemeUniqueGift](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputChatThemeUniqueGift) - Set a theme based on an owned or [hosted collectible gift »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#hosted-collectible-gifts), returned by [account.getUniqueGiftChatThemes](https://core.telegram.org/method/account.getUniqueGiftChatThemes).

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **theme** parameter, removed **emoticon** parameter in [messageActionSetChatTheme](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionSetChatTheme)
* Added **support_email_address**, **support_email_subject** parameters in [auth.sentCodePaymentRequired](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/auth.sentCodePaymentRequired)
* Added **theme** parameter, removed **theme_emoticon** parameter in [userFull](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/userFull)
* Added **theme_available**, **theme_peer** parameters in [starGiftUnique](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftUnique)

#### PUSH notification changes

##### New PUSH notifications

* Added [MESSAGE_STARGIFT_UNPACK_UPGRADE](https://core.telegram.org/api/push-updates#message-stargift-unpack-upgrade) - `{1} unpacked the Gift that you helped upgrade`

#### [File reference map file schema »](https://core.telegram.org/api/file-references)

```
boolFalse#bc799737 = Bool;
boolTrue#997275b5 = Bool;  

true#3fedd339 = True;  

vector#1cb5c415 {t:Type} # [ t ] = Vector t;  

fileReferenceMap#72a99250 layer:int db_schema:string db_schema_json:string traversers_incoming:Vector<TraverserIncoming> traversers_outgoing:Vector<TraverserOutgoing> refresh_actions:Vector<RefreshAction> skipped_incoming_sources:Vector<SkippedSource> = FileReferenceMap;  

traverseParam#62161b67 flags:# name:string is_flag:flags.0?true is_vector:flags.1?true type:string = TraverseParam;
traverseMethodResult#fda9f236 name:string push_sources:Vector<Source> is_needed_parent:Bool = TraverserIncoming;
traverseIncomingConstructor#6e4d00b6 type:string predicate:string params:Vector<TraverseParam> push_sources:Vector<Source> is_needed_parent:Bool = TraverserIncoming;
traverseCommitSourceLocation#94cc59c5 type:string predicate:string push_sources:Vector<Source> stored_constructor:string = TraverserIncoming;  

predicate#de3bc05a predicate:string is_constructor:Bool = Predicate;  

source#4ec4751a flags:# predicate:Predicate stored_constructor:string stored_params:Vector<FieldExtractor> skipped_flags:Vector<string> needs_parent:flags.3?Predicate needs_not_parents:Vector<Predicate> = Source;  

traverseOutgoingConstructor#3fc5026d type:string predicate:string params:Vector<TraverseParam> = TraverserOutgoing;
traverseSwapLocation#032bf95d type:string predicate:string stored_constructor:string = TraverserOutgoing;
traverseMethodCall#9244f5bb name:string params:Vector<TraverseParam> = TraverserOutgoing;  

skippedSource#2b543f6f predicate:Predicate why:string = SkippedSource;  

refreshAction#d4e15689 stored_constructor:string action:ActionOp = RefreshAction;  

paramNotFlag#acd9d5cf = ParamFlag;
paramIsFlagAbortIfEmpty#f8fe9fee = ParamFlag;
paramIsFlagFallback#202b77a1 fallback:TypedOp = ParamFlag;
paramIsFlagPassthrough#1dc6e17d = ParamFlag;
pathPart#19a10fbf type:string constructor:string param:string param_type:string flag:ParamFlag = PathPart;
path#0c3586a2 parts:Vector<PathPart> = Path;
pathParent#58f13684 parts:Vector<PathPart> = Path;  

extractAndStore#72069549 from:Path to:string = FieldExtractor;
extractInputStickerSetFromDocumentAttributesAndStore#369d8d14 from:Path to:string = FieldExtractor;
extractInputStickerSetFromStickerSetAndStore#c167d470 from:Path to:string = FieldExtractor;
extractPeerIdFromPeerAndStore#7d33019c from:Path to:string = FieldExtractor;
extractPeerIdFromInputPeerAndStore#a51acfb4 from:Path to:string = FieldExtractor;
extractChannelIdFromChannelAndStore#5675bc97 from:Path to:string = FieldExtractor;
extractChannelIdFromInputChannelAndStore#b662660e from:Path to:string = FieldExtractor;
extractUserIdFromUserAndStore#4778ec63 from:Path to:string = FieldExtractor;
extractUserIdFromInputUserAndStore#7720aa2e from:Path to:string = FieldExtractor;  

callOp#c2ff3383 method:string args:Vector<TypedOpArg> = ActionOp;  

getMessageOp#8da4c2ae peer:TypedOp id:TypedOp quick_reply_shortcut_id:TypedOp = ActionOp;  

typedOpArg#3a2930c2 key:string value:TypedOp = TypedOpArg;
typedOp#705b10ec type:string op:TypedOpOp = TypedOp;  

copyOp#f48f418f from:string = TypedOpOp;  

getInputChannelByIdOp#3cb47531 from:string = TypedOpOp;
getInputUserByIdOp#c0ee4326 from:string = TypedOpOp;
getInputPeerByIdOp#19813750 from:string = TypedOpOp;  

constructorOp#107f8d8a constructor:string args:Vector<TypedOpArg> = TypedOpOp;  

vectorOp#f8fb8f72 values:Vector<TypedOp> = TypedOpOp;  

intLiteralOp#cbfabe7c value:int = TypedOpOp;  

longLiteralOp#d08b8d3a value:long = TypedOpOp;  

stringLiteralOp#2b56ea8e value:string = TypedOpOp;  

bytesLiteralOp#fdb395a4 value:bytes = TypedOpOp;
boolLiteralOp#37e07911 value:Bool = TypedOpOp;  

doubleLiteralOp#3651e3bf value:double = TypedOpOp;  

themeFormatLiteralOp#8e4f9208 = TypedOpOp;
```

#### [Changes in the file reference database map file »](https://core.telegram.org/api/file-references)

##### New incoming locations

* Added

  ```
  traverseCommitSourceLocation{predicate: document, stored_constructor: fileIdDocument, push_sources: [fileSourceStickerSet, fileSourceSavedMusic (needs users.getSavedMusic), fileSourceSavedMusic (needs users.getSavedMusicByID)], type: Document}
  ```
* Added

  ```
  traverseCommitSourceLocation{predicate: photo, stored_constructor: fileIdPhoto, push_sources: [fileSourceUserProfilePhoto (needs photos.getUserPhotos)], type: Photo}
  ```

##### New outgoing locations

* Added

  ```
  traverseSwapLocation{predicate: inputPhoto, stored_constructor: fileIdPhoto, type: InputPhoto}
  ```
* Added

  ```
  traverseSwapLocation{predicate: inputDocument, stored_constructor: fileIdDocument, type: InputDocument}
  ```
* Added

  ```
  traverseSwapLocation{predicate: inputDocumentFileLocation, stored_constructor: fileIdDocument, type: InputFileLocation}
  ```
* Added

  ```
  traverseSwapLocation{predicate: inputPhotoFileLocation, stored_constructor: fileIdPhoto, type: InputFileLocation}
  ```

##### New sources

* Added (1) [document](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/document) -

  ```
  fileSourceStickerSet{stickerset: extractInputStickerSetFromDocumentAttributesAndStore(document.attributes)}
  ```
* Added (2) [document](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/document) -

  ```
  (needs users.getSavedMusic) fileSourceSavedMusic{user_id: extractUserIdFromInputUserAndStore(users.getSavedMusic.id), id: document.id, access_hash: document.access_hash}
  ```
* Added (3) [document](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/document) -

  ```
  (needs users.getSavedMusicByID) fileSourceSavedMusic{user_id: extractUserIdFromInputUserAndStore(users.getSavedMusicByID.id), id: document.id, access_hash: document.access_hash}
  ```
* Added [photo](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/photo) -

  ```
  (needs photos.getUserPhotos) fileSourceUserProfilePhoto{user_id: extractUserIdFromInputUserAndStore(photos.getUserPhotos.user_id), max_id: photo.id}
  ```
* Added [account.savedRingtoneConverted](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/account.savedRingtoneConverted) -

  ```
  fileSourceSavedRingtones{}
  ```
* Added [account.savedRingtones](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/account.savedRingtones) -

  ```
  fileSourceSavedRingtones{}
  ```
* Added [attachMenuBot](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/attachMenuBot) -

  ```
  fileSourceAttachMenuBot{bot: attachMenuBot.bot_id}
  ```
* Added [botApp](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/botApp) -

  ```
  fileSourceBotApp{id: botApp.id, access_hash: botApp.access_hash}
  ```
* Added [botInfo](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/botInfo) -

  ```
  fileSourceUserFull{id: botInfo.user_id?abort_if_empty}
  ```
* Added [channelAdminLogEvent](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/channelAdminLogEvent) -

  ```
  (needs channels.getAdminLog) fileSourceAdminLog{channel: extractChannelIdFromInputChannelAndStore(channels.getAdminLog.channel), max_id: channelAdminLogEvent.id}
  ```
* Added [channelFull](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/channelFull) -

  ```
  fileSourceChannelFull{channel: channelFull.id}
  ```
* Added [chatFull](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/chatFull) -

  ```
  fileSourceChatFull{chat_id: chatFull.id}
  ```
* Added [foundStory](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/foundStory) -

  ```
  fileSourceStory{id: foundStory.story.storyItem.id, peer: extractPeerIdFromPeerAndStore(foundStory.peer)}
  ```
* Added (1) [message](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/message) -

  ```
  (unless messages.getScheduledMessages, updateNewScheduledMessage) fileSourceMessage{quick_reply_shortcut_id: message.quick_reply_shortcut_id?passthrough, peer: extractPeerIdFromPeerAndStore(message.peer_id), id: message.id}
  ```

##### New skipped incoming sources

* Added [messages.getSponsoredMessages](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.getSponsoredMessages) - Do not store file references from sponsored messages
* Added [help.getAppUpdate](https://core.telegram.org/method/help.getAppUpdate) - Don't handle file references from ephemeral app update info
* Added [help.getRecentMeUrls](https://core.telegram.org/method/help.getRecentMeUrls) - Don't handle file references from recent t.me URLs
* Added [recentMeUrlChatInvite](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/recentMeUrlChatInvite) - Do not store references based on chat invite links
* Added [messages.checkChatInvite](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.checkChatInvite) - Do not store references based on chat invite links
* Added [messages.getInlineBotResults](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.getInlineBotResults) - Inline bot results are ephemeral
* Added [messages.getPreparedInlineMessage](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.getPreparedInlineMessage) - Inline bot results are ephemeral
* Added [messages.uploadMedia](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.uploadMedia) - A freshly uploaded media file will obtain a context only once it is sent to a chat
* Added [messages.uploadImportedMedia](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.uploadImportedMedia) - A freshly uploaded media file will obtain a context only once it is sent to a chat
* Added [updateServiceNotification](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updateServiceNotification) - Cannot refetch service notifications
* Added [messages.getWebPagePreview](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.getWebPagePreview) - No locations are added for the method call, as it doesn't use persistent IDs as input; the location is instead extracted from the persistent IDs in the returned WebPage object
* Added [payments.resaleStarGifts](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/payments.resaleStarGifts) - Contexts for star gifts are not yet implemented
* Added [payments.starGiftUpgradePreview](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/payments.starGiftUpgradePreview) - Contexts for star gifts are not yet implemented
* Added [starGift](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGift) - Contexts for star gifts are not yet implemented
* Added [starGiftUnique](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftUnique) - Contexts for star gifts are not yet implemented
* Added [starGiftCollection](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftCollection) - Contexts for star gifts are not yet implemented
* Added [payments.starGiftCollections](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/payments.starGiftCollections) - Contexts for star gifts are not yet implemented
* Added [messages.getCustomEmojiDocuments](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.getCustomEmojiDocuments) - Do not store file references in this context
* Added [account.uploadTheme](https://core.telegram.org/method/account.uploadTheme) - A freshly uploaded theme file will obtain a context only once it is created via account.createTheme

##### New refresh actions

* Added fileSourceMessage -

  ```
  getMessageOp(peer: getInputPeerByIdOp(peer), id: copyOp(id), quick_reply_shortcut_id: copyOp(quick_reply_shortcut_id))
  ```
* Added fileSourceScheduledMessage -

  ```
  messages.getScheduledMessages(peer: getInputPeerByIdOp(peer), id: [copyOp(id)])
  ```
* Added fileSourceStory -

  ```
  stories.getStoriesByID(id: [copyOp(id)], peer: getInputPeerByIdOp(peer))
  ```
* Added fileSourceWebPage -

  ```
  messages.getWebPage(url: copyOp(url), hash: 0)
  ```
* Added fileSourceBotApp -

  ```
  messages.getBotApp(app: inputBotAppID{id: copyOp(id), access_hash: copyOp(access_hash)}, hash: 0)
  ```
* Added fileSourceUserFull -

  ```
  users.getFullUser(id: getInputUserByIdOp(id))
  ```
* Added fileSourceAdminLog -

  ```
  channels.getAdminLog(channel: getInputChannelByIdOp(channel), max_id: copyOp(max_id), min_id: copyOp(max_id), limit: 1, q: "")
  ```
* Added fileSourceStoryAlbum -

  ```
  stories.getAlbums(peer: getInputPeerByIdOp(peer), hash: 0)
  ```
* Added fileSourceBotPreviewMedia -

  ```
  bots.getPreviewMedias(bot: getInputUserByIdOp(bot))
  ```
* Added fileSourceBotPreviewInfo -

  ```
  bots.getPreviewInfo(bot: getInputUserByIdOp(bot), lang_code: copyOp(lang_code))
  ```
* Added fileSourcePaidMedia -

  ```
  messages.getExtendedMedia(id: [copyOp(id)], peer: getInputPeerByIdOp(peer))
  ```
* Added fileSourceSavedMusic -

  ```
  users.getSavedMusicByID(id: getInputUserByIdOp(user_id), documents: [inputDocument{id: copyOp(id), access_hash: copyOp(access_hash), file_reference: base64_decode("")}])
  ```
* Added fileSourceChatFull -

  ```
  messages.getFullChat(chat_id: copyOp(chat_id))
  ```
* Added fileSourceChannelFull -

  ```
  channels.getFullChannel(channel: getInputChannelByIdOp(channel))
  ```
* Added fileSourcePremiumPromo -

  ```
  help.getPremiumPromo()
  ```
* Added fileSourceStarsTransaction -

  ```
  payments.getStarsTransactionsByID(peer: getInputPeerByIdOp(peer), ton: copyOp(ton), id: [inputStarsTransaction{id: copyOp(id), refund: copyOp(refund)}])
  ```
* Added fileSourceAttachMenuBot -

  ```
  messages.getAttachMenuBot(bot: getInputUserByIdOp(bot))
  ```
* Added fileSourceTheme -

  ```
  account.getTheme(theme: inputTheme{id: copyOp(id), access_hash: copyOp(access_hash)}, format: $themeFormat)
  ```
* Added fileSourceWallPaper -

  ```
  account.getWallPaper(wallpaper: inputWallPaper{id: copyOp(id), access_hash: copyOp(access_hash)})
  ```
* Added fileSourceStickerSet -

  ```
  messages.getStickerSet(stickerset: copyOp(stickerset), hash: 0)
  ```
* Added fileSourceSavedGifs -

  ```
  messages.getSavedGifs(hash: 0)
  ```
* Added fileSourceSavedRingtones -

  ```
  account.getSavedRingtones(hash: 0)
  ```
* Added fileSourceAvailableEffects -

  ```
  messages.getAvailableEffects(hash: 0)
  ```
* Added fileSourceAvailableReactions -

  ```
  messages.getAvailableReactions(hash: 0)
  ```
* Added fileSourceUserProfilePhoto -

  ```
  photos.getUserPhotos(user_id: getInputUserByIdOp(user_id), offset: -1, max_id: copyOp(max_id), limit: 1)
  ```
* Added fileSourceDocumentByHash -

  ```
  messages.getDocumentByHash(sha256: copyOp(sha256), size: copyOp(size), mime_type: copyOp(mime_type))
  ```

### [Layer 213](https://core.telegram.org/schema?layer=213)

This layer introduces the following new features:

* [Changing the default profile tab »](https://core.telegram.org/api/profile#tabs)
* [Saving music to the profile »](https://core.telegram.org/api/profile#music)
* [Added a `locked_until_date` flag to starGift, for locked gifts that cannot be sent until the specified date](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGift).
* [Added the payments.checkCanSendGift method, to check if a non-locked gift can't be sent yet for other reasons](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.checkCanSendGift).
* [Added an optional `spend_purpose_peer` flag to inputStorePaymentStarsTopup, that should be populated with the peer where the topup process was initiated due to low funds (i.e. a bot for bot payments, a channel for paid media/reactions, etc) »](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputStorePaymentStarsTopup)[Documentation for the profile page was also reorganized, to include the full list of tabs to show, along with instructions on how to populate them »](https://core.telegram.org/api/profile).

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [account.setMainProfileTab](https://core.telegram.org/method/account.setMainProfileTab) - Changes the main profile tab of the current user, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/profile#tabs) for more info.
* Added [account.saveMusic](https://core.telegram.org/method/account.saveMusic) - Adds or removes a song from the current user's profile [see here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/profile#music) for more info on the music tab of the profile page.
* Added [account.getSavedMusicIds](https://core.telegram.org/method/account.getSavedMusicIds) - Fetch the full list of only the IDs of [songs currently added to the profile, see here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/profile#music) for more info.
* Added [users.getSavedMusic](https://core.telegram.org/method/users.getSavedMusic) - Get songs [pinned to the user's profile, see here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/profile#music) for more info.
* Added [users.getSavedMusicByID](https://core.telegram.org/method/users.getSavedMusicByID) - Check if the passed songs are still pinned to the user's profile, or refresh the file references of songs pinned on a user's profile [see here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/profile#music) for more info.
* Added [channels.setMainProfileTab](https://core.telegram.org/method/channels.setMainProfileTab) - Changes the main profile tab of a channel, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/profile#tabs) for more info.
* Added [payments.checkCanSendGift](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.checkCanSendGift) - Check if the specified [gift »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts) can be sent.

##### New Constructors

* Added [profileTabPosts](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/profileTabPosts) - Represents the [stories](https://core.telegram.org/api/stories) tab of a profile page.
* Added [profileTabGifts](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/profileTabGifts) - Represents the [gifts](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts) tab of a profile page.
* Added [profileTabMedia](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/profileTabMedia) - Represents the media tab of a profile page.
* Added [profileTabFiles](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/profileTabFiles) - Represents the shared files tab of a profile.
* Added [profileTabMusic](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/profileTabMusic) - Represents the [music](https://core.telegram.org/api/profile#music) tab of a profile page.
* Added [profileTabVoice](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/profileTabVoice) - Represents the voice messages tab of a profile page.
* Added [profileTabLinks](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/profileTabLinks) - Represents the shared links tab of a profile page.
* Added [profileTabGifs](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/profileTabGifs) - Represents the gifs tab of a profile page.
* Added [users.savedMusicNotModified](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/users.savedMusicNotModified) - This subset of the songs currently pinned on a user's profile hasn't changed, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/profile#music) for more info.
* Added [users.savedMusic](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/users.savedMusic) - List of songs currently pinned on a user's profile, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/profile#music) for more info.
* Added [account.savedMusicIdsNotModified](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/account.savedMusicIdsNotModified) - The list of IDs of songs ([document](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/document).`id`s) currently pinned on our profile hasn't changed.
* Added [account.savedMusicIds](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/account.savedMusicIds) - List of IDs of songs ([document](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/document).`id`s) currently pinned on our profile, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/profile#music) for more info.
* Added [payments.checkCanSendGiftResultOk](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/payments.checkCanSendGiftResultOk) - The specified gift can be sent.
* Added [payments.checkCanSendGiftResultFail](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/payments.checkCanSendGiftResultFail) - The specified gift cannot be sent yet for the specified reason.

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **main_tab** parameter in [channelFull](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/channelFull)
* Added **upgrade_separate** parameter in [messageActionStarGift](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionStarGift)
* Added **main_tab**, **saved_music** parameters in [userFull](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/userFull)
* Added **flags**, **spend_purpose_peer** parameters in [inputStorePaymentStarsTopup](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputStorePaymentStarsTopup)
* Added **locked_until_date** parameter in [starGift](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGift)
* Added **chats** parameter in [payments.uniqueStarGift](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/payments.uniqueStarGift)
* Added **chats** parameter in [messages.webPagePreview](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messages.webPagePreview)
* Added **upgrade_separate** parameter in [savedStarGift](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/savedStarGift)

### [Layer 212](https://core.telegram.org/schema?layer=212)

This layer introduces the following features:

* [Separately prepay for the upgrade of a gift »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#prepaying-for-someone-elses-upgrade).
* [Get info about the value of a collectible gift »](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.getUniqueStarGiftValueInfo)

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [payments.getUniqueStarGiftValueInfo](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.getUniqueStarGiftValueInfo) - Get information about the value of a [collectible gift »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gifts).

##### Changed Methods

* Added **exclude_upgradable**, **exclude_unupgradable** parameters, removed **exclude_limited** parameter in [payments.getSavedStarGifts](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.getSavedStarGifts)

##### New Constructors

* Added [inputInvoiceStarGiftPrepaidUpgrade](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputInvoiceStarGiftPrepaidUpgrade) - [Separately prepay for the upgrade of a gift »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#prepaying-for-someone-elses-upgrade).
* Added [payments.uniqueStarGiftValueInfo](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/payments.uniqueStarGiftValueInfo) - Information about the value of a [collectible gift »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gifts).

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **prepaid_upgrade**, **prepaid_upgrade_hash**, **gift_msg_id** parameters in [messageActionStarGift](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionStarGift)
* Added **prepaid_upgrade** parameter in [messageActionStarGiftUnique](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionStarGiftUnique)
* Added **posts_search**, **stargift_prepaid_upgrade** parameters in [starsTransaction](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starsTransaction)
* Added **gift_id**, **value_amount**, **value_currency** parameters in [starGiftUnique](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftUnique)
* Added **prepaid_upgrade_hash** parameter in [savedStarGift](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/savedStarGift)

#### [End-to-end group call schema »](https://core.telegram.org/api/end-to-end/group-calls) changes

##### New Constructors

* Added [e2e.chain.groupBroadcastNonceCommit](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/e2e.chain.groupBroadcastNonceCommit) - Commit message for the [E2E conference call emoji verification protocol](https://core.telegram.org/api/end-to-end/group-calls#commit-reveal-protocol-workflow).
* Added [e2e.chain.groupBroadcastNonceReveal](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/e2e.chain.groupBroadcastNonceReveal) - Reveal message for the [E2E conference call emoji verification protocol](https://core.telegram.org/api/end-to-end/group-calls#commit-reveal-protocol-workflow).
* Added [e2e.chain.groupParticipant](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/e2e.chain.groupParticipant) - A participant and their permissions in an [E2E conference call group state »](https://core.telegram.org/api/end-to-end/group-calls#participants-and-permissions).
* Added [e2e.chain.groupState](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/e2e.chain.groupState) - The participant list and external permission mask for an [E2E conference call blockchain state »](https://core.telegram.org/api/end-to-end/group-calls#participants-and-permissions).
* Added [e2e.chain.sharedKey](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/e2e.chain.sharedKey) - Encrypted [shared group key material](https://core.telegram.org/api/end-to-end/group-calls#shared-key-encryption) for an E2E conference call.
* Added [e2e.chain.changeNoop](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/e2e.chain.changeNoop) - A no-operation [E2E conference call blockchain change](https://core.telegram.org/api/end-to-end/group-calls#change-types-for-group-calls), potentially used only to randomize the resulting block hash.
* Added [e2e.chain.changeSetValue](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/e2e.chain.changeSetValue) - Updates the [key-value trie state for an E2E conference call blockchain](https://core.telegram.org/api/end-to-end/group-calls#change-types-for-group-calls).
* Added [e2e.chain.changeSetGroupState](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/e2e.chain.changeSetGroupState) - Updates the [E2E conference call participant list and permissions](https://core.telegram.org/api/end-to-end/group-calls#change-types-for-group-calls), clearing the current shared key state.
* Added [e2e.chain.changeSetSharedKey](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/e2e.chain.changeSetSharedKey) - Establishes a new shared encryption key for an E2E conference call, see [change types for group calls »](https://core.telegram.org/api/end-to-end/group-calls#change-types-for-group-calls).
* Added [e2e.chain.stateProof](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/e2e.chain.stateProof) - Proof of the [E2E conference call blockchain state](https://core.telegram.org/api/end-to-end/group-calls#block-application-process) after a block has been applied.
* Added [e2e.chain.block](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/e2e.chain.block) - A block in the [main E2E conference call blockchain](https://core.telegram.org/api/end-to-end/group-calls#block-structure), containing state changes for the call and the resulting state proof.
* Added [e2e.callPacket](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/e2e.callPacket) - Marker constructor whose CRC32 constructor ID is used when deriving and authenticating [encrypted group call packets](https://core.telegram.org/api/end-to-end/group-calls#packet-encryption).
* Added [e2e.callPacketLargeMsgId](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/e2e.callPacketLargeMsgId) - Marker constructor whose CRC32 constructor ID is used when signing the large message ID produced while encrypting [group call packets](https://core.telegram.org/api/end-to-end/group-calls#packet-encryption).

#### [End-to-end group call schema »](https://core.telegram.org/api/end-to-end/group-calls)

```
e2e.callPacket#40a6bee9 = e2e.CallPacket;
```

### [Layer 211](https://core.telegram.org/schema?layer=211)

This layer introduces:

* [Story albums »](https://core.telegram.org/api/stories#story-albums)
* [Star gift collection link previews »](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/webPageAttributeStarGiftCollection)
* [Full text global channel post search »](https://core.telegram.org/api/search#posts-tab)
* [Allow specification of the resale price of a collectible gift in TON »](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionStarGiftUnique)
* [Pending star ratings »](https://core.telegram.org/api/stars#star-rating)

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [channels.checkSearchPostsFlood](https://core.telegram.org/method/channels.checkSearchPostsFlood) - Check if the specified [global post search »](https://core.telegram.org/api/search#posts-tab) requires payment.
* Added [stories.createAlbum](https://core.telegram.org/method/stories.createAlbum) - Creates a [story album](https://core.telegram.org/api/stories#story-albums).
* Added [stories.updateAlbum](https://core.telegram.org/method/stories.updateAlbum) - Rename a [story albums »](https://core.telegram.org/api/stories#story-albums), or add, delete or reorder stories in it.
* Added [stories.reorderAlbums](https://core.telegram.org/method/stories.reorderAlbums) - Reorder [story albums on a profile »](https://core.telegram.org/api/stories#story-albums).
* Added [stories.deleteAlbum](https://core.telegram.org/method/stories.deleteAlbum) - Delete a [story album](https://core.telegram.org/api/stories#story-albums).
* Added [stories.getAlbums](https://core.telegram.org/method/stories.getAlbums) - Get [story albums](https://core.telegram.org/api/stories#story-albums) created by a peer.
* Added [stories.getAlbumStories](https://core.telegram.org/method/stories.getAlbumStories) - Get stories in a [story album »](https://core.telegram.org/api/stories#story-albums).

##### Changed Methods

* Added **flags**, **query**, **allow_paid_stars** parameters, changed type of **hashtag** from **string** to **flags.0?string** in [channels.searchPosts](https://core.telegram.org/method/channels.searchPosts)
* Added **resell_amount** parameter, removed **resell_stars** parameter in [payments.updateStarGiftPrice](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.updateStarGiftPrice)
* Added **albums** parameter in [stories.sendStory](https://core.telegram.org/method/stories.sendStory)

##### New Constructors

* Added [webPageAttributeStarGiftCollection](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/webPageAttributeStarGiftCollection) - Contains info about a [gift collection »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#gift-collections) for a [webPage](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/webPage) preview of a [gift collection »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#gift-collections) (the [webPage](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/webPage) will have a `type` of `telegram_collection`).
* Added [storyAlbum](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/storyAlbum) - Represents a [story album »](https://core.telegram.org/api/stories#story-albums).
* Added [stories.albumsNotModified](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/stories.albumsNotModified) - The [story album list »](https://core.telegram.org/api/stories#story-albums) hasn't changed.
* Added [stories.albums](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/stories.albums) - [Story albums »](https://core.telegram.org/api/stories#story-albums).
* Added [searchPostsFlood](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/searchPostsFlood) - Indicates if the specified [global post search »](https://core.telegram.org/api/search#posts-tab) requires payment.

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **resale_amount** parameter, removed **resale_stars** parameter in [messageActionStarGiftUnique](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionStarGiftUnique)
* Added **stars_my_pending_rating**, **stars_my_pending_rating_date** parameters in [userFull](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/userFull)
* Added **search_flood** parameter in [messages.messagesSlice](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messages.messagesSlice)
* Added **flags**, **ton** parameters in [inputInvoiceStarGiftResale](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputInvoiceStarGiftResale)
* Added **albums** parameter in [storyItem](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/storyItem)
* Added **resale_ton_only**, **resell_amount** parameters, removed **resell_stars** parameter in [starGiftUnique](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftUnique)

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema)

### [Layer 210](https://core.telegram.org/schema?layer=210)

This layer introduces:

* [Star gift collections »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#gift-collections)
* Premium-only gifts (`require_premium`) and per-user gift limits for certain gift types (`per_user_total`, `per_user_remains`).
* [Star ratings »](https://core.telegram.org/api/stars#star-rating)

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [payments.createStarGiftCollection](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.createStarGiftCollection) - Create a [star gift collection »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#gift-collections).
* Added [payments.updateStarGiftCollection](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.updateStarGiftCollection) - Add or remove gifts from a [star gift collection »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#gift-collections), or rename the collection.
* Added [payments.reorderStarGiftCollections](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.reorderStarGiftCollections) - Reorder the [star gift collections »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#gift-collections) on an owned peer's profile.
* Added [payments.deleteStarGiftCollection](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.deleteStarGiftCollection) - Delete a [star gift collection »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#gift-collections).
* Added [payments.getStarGiftCollections](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.getStarGiftCollections) - Fetches all [star gift collections »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#gift-collections) of a peer.

##### Changed Methods

* Added **collection_id** parameter in [payments.getSavedStarGifts](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.getSavedStarGifts)

##### New Constructors

* Added [starsRating](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starsRating) - Represents the profile's [star rating, see here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/stars#star-rating) for more info.
* Added [starGiftCollection](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftCollection) - Represents a [star gift collection »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#gift-collections).
* Added [payments.starGiftCollectionsNotModified](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/payments.starGiftCollectionsNotModified) - The list of [star gift collections »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#gift-collections) hasn't changed.
* Added [payments.starGiftCollections](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/payments.starGiftCollections) - Represents a list of [star gift collections »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#gift-collections).

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **stars_rating** parameter in [userFull](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/userFull)
* Added **require_premium**, **limited_per_user**, **per_user_total**, **per_user_remains** parameters in [starGift](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGift)
* Added **require_premium** parameter in [starGiftUnique](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftUnique)
* Added **collection_id** parameter in [savedStarGift](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/savedStarGift)

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema)

```
userFull#29de80be flags:# blocked:flags.0?true phone_calls_available:flags.4?true phone_calls_private:flags.5?true can_pin_message:flags.7?true has_scheduled:flags.12?true video_calls_available:flags.13?true voice_messages_forbidden:flags.20?true translations_disabled:flags.23?true stories_pinned_available:flags.26?true blocked_my_stories_from:flags.27?true wallpaper_overridden:flags.28?true contact_require_premium:flags.29?true read_dates_private:flags.30?true flags2:# sponsored_enabled:flags2.7?true can_view_revenue:flags2.9?true bot_can_manage_emoji_status:flags2.10?true display_gifts_button:flags2.16?true id:long about:flags.1?string settings:PeerSettings personal_photo:flags.21?Photo profile_photo:flags.2?Photo fallback_photo:flags.22?Photo notify_settings:PeerNotifySettings bot_info:flags.3?BotInfo pinned_msg_id:flags.6?int common_chats_count:int folder_id:flags.11?int ttl_period:flags.14?int theme_emoticon:flags.15?string private_forward_name:flags.16?string bot_group_admin_rights:flags.17?ChatAdminRights bot_broadcast_admin_rights:flags.18?ChatAdminRights wallpaper:flags.24?WallPaper stories:flags.25?PeerStories business_work_hours:flags2.0?BusinessWorkHours business_location:flags2.1?BusinessLocation business_greeting_message:flags2.2?BusinessGreetingMessage business_away_message:flags2.3?BusinessAwayMessage business_intro:flags2.4?BusinessIntro birthday:flags2.5?Birthday personal_channel_id:flags2.6?long personal_channel_message:flags2.6?int stargifts_count:flags2.8?int starref_program:flags2.11?StarRefProgram bot_verification:flags2.12?BotVerification send_paid_messages_stars:flags2.14?long disallowed_gifts:flags2.15?DisallowedGiftsSettings stars_rating:flags2.17?StarsRating = UserFull;
```

### [Layer 208](https://core.telegram.org/schema?layer=208)

This layer introduces:

* [Suggested channel posts »](https://core.telegram.org/api/suggested-posts)
* Add a new `manage_direct_messages` [admin right](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/chatAdminRights)
* Add support for the new `manage_direct_messages` [admin right](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/chatAdminRights) in [group/channel bot links](https://core.telegram.org/api/links#group-channel-bot-links)
* [Allow replying to specific todo tasks »](https://core.telegram.org/api/todo)
* Unify channel revenue statistics and withdrawal methods with the equivalent ones used for stars, by simply adding a `ton` flag and the missing parameters:
  + [Balance and transaction history »](https://core.telegram.org/api/stars#balance-and-transaction-history)
  + [Revenue statistics »](https://core.telegram.org/api/stars#revenue-statistics)
  + [Withdrawing revenue »](https://core.telegram.org/api/stars#withdrawing-revenue)
* In some places in the API, it's possible to use Grams instead of Stars, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/stars#gram) for more info.

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema) changes

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **todo_item_id** parameter in [messageReplyHeader](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageReplyHeader)
* Added **todo_item_id** parameter in [inputReplyToMessage](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputReplyToMessage)
* Removed **posts_search** parameter in [starsTransaction](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starsTransaction)

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema)

```
messageReplyHeader#6917560b flags:# reply_to_scheduled:flags.2?true forum_topic:flags.3?true quote:flags.9?true reply_to_msg_id:flags.4?int reply_to_peer_id:flags.0?Peer reply_from:flags.5?MessageFwdHeader reply_media:flags.8?MessageMedia reply_to_top_id:flags.1?int quote_text:flags.6?string quote_entities:flags.7?Vector<MessageEntity> quote_offset:flags.10?int todo_item_id:flags.11?int = MessageReplyHeader;  

inputReplyToMessage#869fbe10 flags:# reply_to_msg_id:int top_msg_id:flags.0?int reply_to_peer_id:flags.1?InputPeer quote_text:flags.2?string quote_entities:flags.3?Vector<MessageEntity> quote_offset:flags.4?int monoforum_peer_id:flags.5?InputPeer todo_item_id:flags.6?int = InputReplyTo;  

starsTransaction#13659eb0 flags:# refund:flags.3?true pending:flags.4?true failed:flags.6?true gift:flags.10?true reaction:flags.11?true stargift_upgrade:flags.18?true business_transfer:flags.21?true stargift_resale:flags.22?true id:string amount:StarsAmount date:int peer:StarsTransactionPeer title:flags.0?string description:flags.1?string photo:flags.2?WebDocument transaction_date:flags.5?int transaction_url:flags.5?string bot_payload:flags.7?bytes msg_id:flags.8?int extended_media:flags.9?Vector<MessageMedia> subscription_period:flags.12?int giveaway_post_id:flags.13?int stargift:flags.14?StarGift floodskip_number:flags.15?int starref_commission_permille:flags.16?int starref_peer:flags.17?Peer starref_amount:flags.17?StarsAmount paid_messages:flags.19?int premium_gift_months:flags.20?int ads_proceeds_from_date:flags.23?int ads_proceeds_to_date:flags.23?int = StarsTransaction;
```

### [Layer 207](https://core.telegram.org/schema?layer=207)

This layer introduces:

* [Suggested posts »](https://core.telegram.org/api/suggested-posts), letting users propose paid posts to channels, with approval, success and refund [service messages](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionSuggestedPostApproval)
* A new `manage_direct_messages` [admin right](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/chatAdminRights)
* The ability to gift, hold and withdraw [Gram »](https://core.telegram.org/api/stars#gram) alongside Telegram Stars: TON balances and transactions in the stars status/transaction/revenue methods, TON gifts ([messageActionGiftTon](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionGiftTon), [starsTonAmount](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starsTonAmount)), and the unification of channel revenue statistics and withdrawals with the equivalent Stars methods
* [Global post search »](https://core.telegram.org/api/search#posts-tab)

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [messages.toggleSuggestedPostApproval](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.toggleSuggestedPostApproval) - Approve or reject a [suggested post »](https://core.telegram.org/api/suggested-posts).

##### Changed Methods

* Added **suggested_post** parameter in [messages.sendMessage](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.sendMessage)
* Added **suggested_post** parameter in [messages.sendMedia](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.sendMedia)
* Added **suggested_post** parameter in [messages.forwardMessages](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.forwardMessages)
* Added **suggested_post** parameter in [messages.saveDraft](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.saveDraft)
* Added **flags**, **ton** parameters in [payments.getStarsStatus](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.getStarsStatus)
* Added **ton** parameter in [payments.getStarsTransactions](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.getStarsTransactions)
* Added **ton** parameter in [payments.getStarsRevenueStats](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.getStarsRevenueStats)
* Added **flags**, **ton**, **amount** parameters, removed **stars** parameter in [payments.getStarsRevenueWithdrawalUrl](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.getStarsRevenueWithdrawalUrl)
* Added **flags**, **ton** parameters in [payments.getStarsTransactionsByID](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.getStarsTransactionsByID)

##### Deleted Methods

* Removed stats.getBroadcastRevenueStats
* Removed stats.getBroadcastRevenueWithdrawalUrl
* Removed stats.getBroadcastRevenueTransactions

##### New Constructors

* Added [suggestedPost](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/suggestedPost) - Contains info about a [suggested post »](https://core.telegram.org/api/suggested-posts).
* Added [messageActionSuggestedPostApproval](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionSuggestedPostApproval) - A [suggested post »](https://core.telegram.org/api/suggested-posts) was approved or rejected.
* Added [messageActionSuggestedPostSuccess](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionSuggestedPostSuccess) - A [suggested post »](https://core.telegram.org/api/suggested-posts) was successfully posted, and payment for it was successfully received.
* Added [messageActionSuggestedPostRefund](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionSuggestedPostRefund) - A [suggested post »](https://core.telegram.org/api/suggested-posts) was accepted and posted or scheduled, but either the channel deleted the posted/scheduled post before [stars_suggested_post_age_min](https://core.telegram.org/api/config#stars-suggested-post-age-min) seconds have elapsed, or the user refunded the payment for the stars used to pay for the suggested post.
* Added [starsTonAmount](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starsTonAmount) - Describes an amount of Gram in nanograms (i.e. `1/1_000_000_000` of a Gram).
* Added [messageActionGiftTon](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionGiftTon) - You were gifted some Grams.
* Added [inputStickerSetTonGifts](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputStickerSetTonGifts) - TON gifts stickerset.

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **paid_suggested_post_stars**, **paid_suggested_post_ton**, **suggested_post** parameters in [message](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/message)
* Added **suggested_post** parameter in [draftMessage](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/draftMessage)
* Added **manage_direct_messages** parameter in [chatAdminRights](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/chatAdminRights)
* Added **posts_search**, **amount**, **ads_proceeds_from_date**, **ads_proceeds_to_date** parameters, removed **stars** parameter in [starsTransaction](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starsTransaction)
* Added **flags**, **top_hours_graph** parameters in [payments.starsRevenueStats](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/payments.starsRevenueStats)
* Added **released_by** parameter in [starGift](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGift)
* Added **chats**, **users** parameters in [payments.starGifts](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/payments.starGifts)
* Added **released_by** parameter in [starGiftUnique](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftUnique)

##### Deleted Constructors

* Removed stats.broadcastRevenueStats
* Removed stats.broadcastRevenueWithdrawalUrl
* Removed broadcastRevenueTransactionProceeds
* Removed broadcastRevenueTransactionWithdrawal
* Removed broadcastRevenueTransactionRefund
* Removed stats.broadcastRevenueTransactions
* Removed broadcastRevenueBalances
* Removed updateBroadcastRevenueTransactions

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema)

