### [Layer 205](https://core.telegram.org/schema?layer=205)

This layer introduces:

* [Sponsored messages to show on channel videos »](https://core.telegram.org/api/sponsored-messages#getting-sponsored-video-advertisements)
* [To-do lists »](https://core.telegram.org/api/todo)
* [Rename account.addNoPaidMessagesException to account.toggleNoPaidMessagesException, and add a `require_payment` flag to refund all Stars transferred to us by a peer with paid messages »](https://core.telegram.org/method/account.toggleNoPaidMessagesException)
* Allow invoking [account.toggleNoPaidMessagesException](https://core.telegram.org/method/account.toggleNoPaidMessagesException) for monoforum topics, and add a new [updateMonoForumNoPaidException](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updateMonoForumNoPaidException) to signal changes to [monoForumDialog](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/monoForumDialog).`nopaid_messages_exception` to other admins and to other currently logged in sessions of the current monoforum admin.

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [messages.toggleTodoCompleted](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.toggleTodoCompleted) - Mark one or more items of a [todo list »](https://core.telegram.org/api/todo) as completed or not completed.
* Added [messages.appendTodoList](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.appendTodoList) - Appends one or more items to a [todo list »](https://core.telegram.org/api/todo).
* Added [account.toggleNoPaidMessagesException](https://core.telegram.org/method/account.toggleNoPaidMessagesException) - Allow a user to send us messages without paying if [paid messages »](https://core.telegram.org/api/paid-messages) are enabled.

##### Changed Methods

* Added **flags**, **msg_id** parameters in [messages.getSponsoredMessages](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.getSponsoredMessages)
* Added **flags**, **parent_peer** parameters in [account.getPaidMessagesRevenue](https://core.telegram.org/method/account.getPaidMessagesRevenue)

##### Deleted Methods

* Removed account.addNoPaidMessagesException

##### New Constructors

* Added [todoItem](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/todoItem) - An item of a [todo list »](https://core.telegram.org/api/todo).
* Added [todoList](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/todoList) - Represents a [todo list »](https://core.telegram.org/api/todo).
* Added [todoCompletion](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/todoCompletion) - A completed [todo list »](https://core.telegram.org/api/todo) item.
* Added [inputMediaTodo](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputMediaTodo) - Creates a [todo list »](https://core.telegram.org/api/todo).
* Added [messageMediaToDo](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageMediaToDo) - Represents a [todo list »](https://core.telegram.org/api/todo).
* Added [messageActionTodoCompletions](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionTodoCompletions) - Items were marked as completed or not completed in a [todo list »](https://core.telegram.org/api/todo).
* Added [messageActionTodoAppendTasks](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionTodoAppendTasks) - Items were appended to the [todo list »](https://core.telegram.org/api/todo).
* Added [updateMonoForumNoPaidException](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updateMonoForumNoPaidException) - An admin has (un)exempted this [monoforum topic »](https://core.telegram.org/api/monoforum) from payment to send messages using [account.toggleNoPaidMessagesException](https://core.telegram.org/method/account.toggleNoPaidMessagesException).

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **saved_peer_id** parameter in [messageService](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageService)
* Added **send_paid_messages_stars** parameter in [channelFull](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/channelFull)
* Added **min_display_duration**, **max_display_duration** parameters in [sponsoredMessage](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/sponsoredMessage)
* Added **start_delay**, **between_delay** parameters in [messages.sponsoredMessages](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messages.sponsoredMessages)
* Added **nopaid_messages_exception** parameter in [monoForumDialog](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/monoForumDialog)

#### PUSH notification changes

##### New PUSH notifications

* Added [CHANNEL_MESSAGE_TODO](https://core.telegram.org/api/push-updates#channel-message-todo) - `{1} posted a checklist {2}`
* Added [CHANNEL_MESSAGE_TODO_APPEND](https://core.telegram.org/api/push-updates#channel-message-todo-append) - `{1} added {2} tasks`
* Added [CHANNEL_MESSAGE_TODO_DONE](https://core.telegram.org/api/push-updates#channel-message-todo-done) - `{1} toggled {2} tasks`
* Added [CHAT_MESSAGE_TODO](https://core.telegram.org/api/push-updates#chat-message-todo) - `{1} sent a checklist {3} to the group {2}`
* Added [CHAT_MESSAGE_TODO_APPEND](https://core.telegram.org/api/push-updates#chat-message-todo-append) - `{1} added {3} tasks in the group {2}`
* Added [CHAT_MESSAGE_TODO_DONE](https://core.telegram.org/api/push-updates#chat-message-todo-done) - `{1} toggled {3} tasks in the group {2}`
* Added [CHAT_REACT_TODO](https://core.telegram.org/api/push-updates#chat-react-todo) - `{1}: {3} to your checklist {4} in {2}`
* Added [MESSAGE_TODO](https://core.telegram.org/api/push-updates#message-todo) - `{1} sent you a checklist {2}`
* Added [PINNED_TODO](https://core.telegram.org/api/push-updates#pinned-todo) - `{1} pinned a checklist {2}`
* Added [REACT_TODO](https://core.telegram.org/api/push-updates#react-todo) - `{1}: {2} to your checklist {3}`

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema)

```
messageService#7a800e0a flags:# out:flags.1?true mentioned:flags.4?true media_unread:flags.5?true reactions_are_possible:flags.9?true silent:flags.13?true post:flags.14?true legacy:flags.19?true id:int from_id:flags.8?Peer peer_id:Peer saved_peer_id:flags.28?Peer reply_to:flags.3?MessageReplyHeader date:int action:MessageAction reactions:flags.20?MessageReactions ttl_period:flags.25?int = Message;

### [Layer 204](https://core.telegram.org/schema?layer=204)

This layer introduces:

* [Monoforums »](https://core.telegram.org/api/monoforum)
* [Bot API monoforum IDs »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/ids#monoforum-ids)
* [Tabbed forum UI »](https://core.telegram.org/api/forum#tabbed-or-list-based-forum-ui)
* All flags added to [channel](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/channel) (listed below) are valid `min` flags, meaning they must be applied over the locally stored version even if the `min` flag of the incoming [channel](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/channel) is set.

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [messages.getSavedDialogsByID](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.getSavedDialogsByID) - Obtain information about specific [saved message dialogs »](https://core.telegram.org/api/saved-messages#saved-message-dialogs) or [monoforum topics »](https://core.telegram.org/api/monoforum).
* Added [messages.readSavedHistory](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.readSavedHistory) - Mark messages as read in a [monoforum topic »](https://core.telegram.org/api/monoforum).
* Added [channels.getMessageAuthor](https://core.telegram.org/method/channels.getMessageAuthor) - Can only be invoked by non-bot admins of a [monoforum »](https://core.telegram.org/api/monoforum), obtains the original sender of a message sent by other monoforum admins to the monoforum, on behalf of the channel associated to the monoforum.

##### Changed Methods

* Added **reply_to** parameter in [messages.forwardMessages](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.forwardMessages)
* Added **parent_peer** parameter in [messages.markDialogUnread](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.markDialogUnread)
* Added **flags**, **parent_peer** parameters in [messages.getDialogUnreadMarks](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.getDialogUnreadMarks)
* Added **saved_peer_id** parameter in [messages.unpinAllMessages](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.unpinAllMessages)
* Added **saved_peer_id** parameter in [messages.getUnreadReactions](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.getUnreadReactions)
* Added **saved_peer_id** parameter in [messages.readReactions](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.readReactions)
* Added **tabs** parameter in [channels.toggleForum](https://core.telegram.org/method/channels.toggleForum)
* Added **parent_peer** parameter in [messages.getSavedDialogs](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.getSavedDialogs)
* Added **flags**, **parent_peer** parameters in [messages.getSavedHistory](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.getSavedHistory)
* Added **parent_peer** parameter in [messages.deleteSavedHistory](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.deleteSavedHistory)
* Added **flags**, **broadcast_messages_allowed** parameters in [channels.updatePaidMessagesPrice](https://core.telegram.org/method/channels.updatePaidMessagesPrice)

##### New Constructors

* Added [inputReplyToMonoForum](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputReplyToMonoForum) - Used to send messages to a [monoforum topic](https://core.telegram.org/api/monoforum).
* Added [monoForumDialog](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/monoForumDialog) - Represents a [monoforum topic »](https://core.telegram.org/api/monoforum).
* Added [updateReadMonoForumInbox](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updateReadMonoForumInbox) - Incoming messages in a [monoforum topic](https://core.telegram.org/api/monoforum) were read
* Added [updateReadMonoForumOutbox](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updateReadMonoForumOutbox) - Outgoing messages in a [monoforum](https://core.telegram.org/api/monoforum) were read.

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **broadcast_messages_allowed**, **monoforum**, **forum_tabs**, **linked_monoforum_id** parameters in [channel](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/channel)
* Added **saved_peer_id** parameter in [updateDraftMessage](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updateDraftMessage)
* Added **saved_peer_id** parameter in [updateChannelReadMessagesContents](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updateChannelReadMessagesContents)
* Added **saved_peer_id** parameter in [updateDialogUnreadMark](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updateDialogUnreadMark)
* Added **saved_peer_id** parameter in [updateMessageReactions](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updateMessageReactions)
* Added **monoforum_peer_id** parameter in [inputReplyToMessage](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputReplyToMessage)
* Added **flags**, **broadcast_messages_allowed** parameters in [messageActionPaidMessagesPrice](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionPaidMessagesPrice)

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema)

```

### [Layer 203](https://core.telegram.org/schema?layer=203)

This layer introduces:

* [Methods to resell collectible gifts »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#reselling-collectible-gifts)
* [Autotranslation for all users of a channel »](https://core.telegram.org/api/translation#autotranslation-for-channels)
* [stories.canSendStory now returns the number of available active story slots »](https://core.telegram.org/method/stories.canSendStory)
* [Collectible gift links »](https://core.telegram.org/api/links#collectible-gift-link)
* [Custom suggestions »](https://core.telegram.org/api/config#custom-suggestions)
* [All suggestion fields were moved from the client configuration object to help.getPromoData »](https://core.telegram.org/api/config#suggestions)
* All flags added to [channel](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/channel) (listed below) are valid `min` flags, meaning they must be applied over the locally stored version even if the `min` flag of the incoming [channel](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/channel) is set.

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [payments.getResaleStarGifts](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.getResaleStarGifts) - Get [collectible gifts](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gifts) of a specific type currently on resale, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#reselling-collectible-gifts) for more info.
* Added [payments.updateStarGiftPrice](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.updateStarGiftPrice) - A [collectible gift we own »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gifts) can be put up for sale on the [gift marketplace »](https://telegram.org/blog/gift-marketplace-and-more) with this method, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#reselling-collectible-gifts) for more info.
* Added [channels.toggleAutotranslation](https://core.telegram.org/method/channels.toggleAutotranslation) - Toggle autotranslation in a channel, for all users: see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/translation#autotranslation-for-channels) for more info.

##### Changed Methods

* Changed type of [stories.canSendStory](https://core.telegram.org/method/stories.canSendStory) from **Bool** to **stories.CanSendStoryCount**

##### New Constructors

* Added [inputSavedStarGiftSlug](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputSavedStarGiftSlug) - Points to a [collectible gift obtained from a collectible gift link »](https://core.telegram.org/api/links#collectible-gift-link).
* Added [starGiftAttributeIdModel](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftAttributeIdModel) - The ID of a model of a [collectible gift »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gifts).
* Added [starGiftAttributeIdPattern](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftAttributeIdPattern) - The ID of a pattern of a [collectible gift »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gifts).
* Added [starGiftAttributeIdBackdrop](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftAttributeIdBackdrop) - The ID of a backdrop of a [collectible gift »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gifts).
* Added [starGiftAttributeCounter](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftAttributeCounter) - Indicates the total number of gifts that have the specified attribute.
* Added [payments.resaleStarGifts](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/payments.resaleStarGifts) - List of gifts currently on [resale »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#reselling-collectible-gifts).
* Added [inputInvoiceStarGiftResale](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputInvoiceStarGiftResale) - Used to buy a [collectible gift](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gifts) currently up on resale, see [here](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#reselling-collectible-gifts) for more info on the full flow.
* Added [channelAdminLogEventActionToggleAutotranslation](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/channelAdminLogEventActionToggleAutotranslation) - [Channel autotranslation was toggled »](https://core.telegram.org/api/translation#autotranslation-for-channels).
* Added [stories.canSendStoryCount](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/stories.canSendStoryCount) - Contains the number of available active story slots (equal to the value of the [`story_expiring_limit_*` client configuration parameter](https://core.telegram.org/api/config#story-expiring-limit-default) minus the number of currently active stories).
* Added [pendingSuggestion](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/pendingSuggestion) - Represents a [custom pending suggestion »](https://core.telegram.org/api/config#custom-suggestions).

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **autotranslation** parameter in [channel](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/channel)
* Added **pending_suggestions**, **dismissed_suggestions**, **custom_pending_suggestion** parameters, changed type of **peer** from **Peer** to **flags.3?Peer** in [help.promoData](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/help.promoData)
* Added **availability_resale**, **resell_min_stars**, **title** parameters in [starGift](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGift)
* Added **backdrop_id** parameter in [starGiftAttributeBackdrop](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftAttributeBackdrop)
* Added **resell_stars** parameter in [starGiftUnique](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftUnique)
* Added **resale_stars**, **can_transfer_at**, **can_resell_at** parameters in [messageActionStarGiftUnique](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionStarGiftUnique)
* Added **can_transfer_at**, **can_resell_at** parameters in [savedStarGift](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/savedStarGift)

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema)

```

channels.toggleAutotranslation#167fc0a1 channel:InputChannel enabled:Bool = Updates;

### [Layer 202](https://core.telegram.org/schema?layer=202)

This layer introduces:

* [End-to-end group calls »](https://core.telegram.org/api/end-to-end/group-calls)
  + [Conference deep links »](https://core.telegram.org/api/links#conference-links)
* [Service messages for paid message refunds »](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionPaidMessagesRefunded)
* [Service messages for paid message price changes »](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionPaidMessagesPrice)
* Gift privacy settings:
  + [globalPrivacySettings](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/globalPrivacySettings).`disallowed_gifts`/[userFull](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/userFull).`disallowed_gifts`: [Disallow the reception of specific gift types »](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/disallowedGiftsSettings)
  + [globalPrivacySettings](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/globalPrivacySettings).`display_gifts_button`/[userFull](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/userFull).`display_gifts_button`: show or hide the gift button in the text field of private chats.
* [Transferring stars from a business account to the business bot »](https://core.telegram.org/api/stars#transferring-stars-from-a-business-account-to-the-business-bot)
* [Sponsored peer search results »](https://core.telegram.org/api/sponsored-messages#getting-sponsored-search-results)
* [Business bot rights »](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/businessBotRights)
* [Sponsored message methods now don't require specification of the `peer` where the sponsored message is visible »](https://core.telegram.org/api/sponsored-messages)
* [Renamed the method used by official apps to check whether an in-store purchase is possible »](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.canPurchaseStore)
* [Paid auth codes for official apps](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/auth.sentCodePaymentRequired)

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [payments.canPurchaseStore](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.canPurchaseStore) - Checks whether a purchase is possible. Must be called before in-store purchase, official apps only.
* Added [contacts.getSponsoredPeers](https://core.telegram.org/method/contacts.getSponsoredPeers) - Obtain a list of sponsored peer search results for a given query
* Added [phone.deleteConferenceCallParticipants](https://core.telegram.org/method/phone.deleteConferenceCallParticipants) - Remove participants from a [conference call »](https://core.telegram.org/api/end-to-end/group-calls#removing-a-participant).
* Added [phone.sendConferenceCallBroadcast](https://core.telegram.org/method/phone.sendConferenceCallBroadcast) - Submit a verification message to conference call subchain `1`, see [subchains »](https://core.telegram.org/api/end-to-end/group-calls#subchains).
* Added [phone.inviteConferenceCallParticipant](https://core.telegram.org/method/phone.inviteConferenceCallParticipant) - Invite a user to a [conference call](https://core.telegram.org/api/group-calls#conference-calls).
* Added [phone.declineConferenceCallInvite](https://core.telegram.org/method/phone.declineConferenceCallInvite) - Decline a [conference call](https://core.telegram.org/api/group-calls#conference-calls) invite.
* Added [phone.getGroupCallChainBlocks](https://core.telegram.org/method/phone.getGroupCallChainBlocks) - Fetch blocks from a conference call [subchain »](https://core.telegram.org/api/end-to-end/group-calls#subchains); handle the returned [updateGroupCallChainBlocks](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updateGroupCallChainBlocks) as [specified here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/end-to-end/group-calls#handling-updates).

##### Changed Methods

* Removed **conference_call** parameter in [phone.requestCall](https://core.telegram.org/method/phone.requestCall)
* Added **public_key**, **block** parameters, removed **key_fingerprint** parameter in [phone.joinGroupCall](https://core.telegram.org/method/phone.joinGroupCall)
* Added **rights** parameter, removed **can_reply** parameter in [account.updateConnectedBot](https://core.telegram.org/method/account.updateConnectedBot)
* Removed **peer** parameter in [messages.viewSponsoredMessage](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.viewSponsoredMessage)
* Removed **peer** parameter in [messages.clickSponsoredMessage](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.clickSponsoredMessage)
* Removed **peer** parameter in [messages.reportSponsoredMessage](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.reportSponsoredMessage)
* Changed type of [phone.createConferenceCall](https://core.telegram.org/method/phone.createConferenceCall) from **phone.PhoneCall** to **Updates**
* Added **flags**, **muted**, **video_stopped**, **join**, **random_id**, **public_key**, **block**, **params** parameters, removed **peer**, **key_fingerprint** parameters in [phone.createConferenceCall](https://core.telegram.org/method/phone.createConferenceCall)

##### Deleted Methods

* Removed payments.canPurchasePremium

##### New Constructors

* Added [auth.sentCodePaymentRequired](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/auth.sentCodePaymentRequired) - Official apps may receive this constructor, indicating that due to the high cost of SMS verification codes for the user's country/provider, the user must purchase a [Telegram Premium](https://core.telegram.org/api/premium) subscription in order to proceed with the login/signup, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/auth#paid-auth) for more info.
* Added [inputStorePaymentAuthCode](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputStorePaymentAuthCode) - Indicates payment for a login code.
* Added [updateSentPhoneCode](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updateSentPhoneCode) - A paid login SMS code was successfully sent.
* Added [businessBotRights](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/businessBotRights) - [Business bot rights](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/connected-business-bots).
* Added [messageActionPaidMessagesRefunded](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionPaidMessagesRefunded) - Sent from peer A to B, indicates that A refunded all [stars](https://core.telegram.org/api/stars) B previously paid to send messages to A, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/paid-messages) for more info on paid messages.
* Added [messageActionPaidMessagesPrice](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionPaidMessagesPrice) - The price of [paid messages »](https://core.telegram.org/api/paid-messages) in this chat was changed.
* Added [disallowedGiftsSettings](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/disallowedGiftsSettings) - Disallow the reception of specific [gift](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts) types.
* Added [sponsoredPeer](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/sponsoredPeer) - A sponsored peer.
* Added [contacts.sponsoredPeersEmpty](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/contacts.sponsoredPeersEmpty) - There are no sponsored peers for this query.
* Added [contacts.sponsoredPeers](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/contacts.sponsoredPeers) - Sponsored peers.
* Added [inputInvoiceBusinessBotTransferStars](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputInvoiceBusinessBotTransferStars) - Transfer stars from the balance of a user account [connected to a business bot](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/connected-business-bots), to the balance of the [business bot](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/connected-business-bots), see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/stars#transferring-stars-from-a-business-account-to-the-business-bot) for more info on the full flow.
* Added [inputGroupCallSlug](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputGroupCallSlug) - Identify a [conference call »](https://core.telegram.org/api/group-calls#conference-calls) using the slug from its invitation link.
* Added [inputGroupCallInviteMessage](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputGroupCallInviteMessage) - Identifies a [conference call »](https://core.telegram.org/api/group-calls#conference-calls) using its [messageActionConferenceCall](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionConferenceCall) invitation service message.
* Added [updateGroupCallChainBlocks](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updateGroupCallChainBlocks) - Contains conference call blockchain blocks, see [handling E2E group call updates »](https://core.telegram.org/api/end-to-end/group-calls#handling-updates).
* Added [messageActionConferenceCall](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionConferenceCall) - Represents a [conference call »](https://core.telegram.org/api/group-calls#conference-calls), or an invitation to one if neither the `missed` nor `active` flags are set.
* Added [phoneCallDiscardReasonMigrateConferenceCall](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/phoneCallDiscardReasonMigrateConferenceCall) - This phone call was migrated to a [conference call](https://core.telegram.org/api/end-to-end/group-calls).

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **display_gifts_button**, **disallowed_gifts** parameters in [userFull](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/userFull)
* Removed **conference_call** parameter in [phoneCallWaiting](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/phoneCallWaiting)
* Removed **conference_call** parameter in [phoneCallRequested](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/phoneCallRequested)
* Removed **conference_call** parameter in [phoneCallAccepted](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/phoneCallAccepted)
* Added **conference_supported** parameter, removed **conference_call** parameter in [phoneCall](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/phoneCall)
* Removed **conference_call** parameter in [phoneCallDiscarded](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/phoneCallDiscarded)
* Added **display_gifts_button**, **disallowed_gifts** parameters in [globalPrivacySettings](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/globalPrivacySettings)
* Added **conference**, **creator**, **invite_link** parameters, removed **conference_from_call** parameter in [groupCall](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/groupCall)
* Added **rights** parameter, removed **can_reply** parameter in [connectedBot](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/connectedBot)
* Added **rights** parameter, removed **can_reply** parameter in [botBusinessConnection](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/botBusinessConnection)

#### PUSH notification changes

##### New PUSH notifications

* Added [CONF_CALL_MISSED](https://core.telegram.org/api/push-updates#conf-call-missed) - `You missed a call from {1}`

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema)

```

### [Layer 200](https://core.telegram.org/schema?layer=200)

This layer introduces:

* [Detailed account information for new private chats »](https://core.telegram.org/api/action-bar#account-information)
* [Paid messages »](https://core.telegram.org/api/paid-messages)
* [Pinning a specific received gift to the profile »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#pinning-a-received-gift)
* [Sending paid reactions as a channel »](https://core.telegram.org/api/reactions#paid-reaction-privacy)
* [Added a `paid_messages_available` flag to channelFull, indicating whether paid messages can be enabled in this supergroup](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/channelFull)
* [Gift a Telegram Premium subscription, paying with Telegram Stars »](https://core.telegram.org/api/premium#gifting-telegram-premium)  
  This change also simplifies the Telegram Premium gift flow, fully replacing the [userFull](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/userFull).`premium_gifts` flag with [payments.getPremiumGiftCodeOptions](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.getPremiumGiftCodeOptions).
* [Blockchain addresses for collectible gifts moved to the TON blockchain as NFTs »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#withdraw-a-collectible-gift-to-the-ton-blockchain)
* All flags added to [channel](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/channel) (listed below) are valid `min` flags, meaning they must be applied over the locally stored version even if the `min` flag of the incoming [channel](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/channel) is set.
* All flags added to [user](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/user) (listed below) are valid `min` flags, meaning they must be applied over the locally stored version even if the `min` flag of the incoming [user](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/user) is set.

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [invokeWithReCaptcha](https://core.telegram.org/method/invokeWithReCaptcha) - Official clients only: re-execute a method call that required reCAPTCHA verification via a `RECAPTCHA_CHECK_%s__%s`, where the first placeholder is the `action`, and the second one is the reCAPTCHA key ID.
* Added [account.addNoPaidMessagesException](https://core.telegram.org/method/account.addNoPaidMessagesException)
* Added [account.getPaidMessagesRevenue](https://core.telegram.org/method/account.getPaidMessagesRevenue) - Get the number of stars we have received from the specified user thanks to [paid messages »](https://core.telegram.org/api/paid-messages); the received amount will be equal to the sent amount multiplied by [stars_paid_message_commission_permille](https://core.telegram.org/api/config#stars-paid-message-commission-permille) divided by 1000.
* Added [channels.updatePaidMessagesPrice](https://core.telegram.org/method/channels.updatePaidMessagesPrice) - Enable or disable [paid messages »](https://core.telegram.org/api/paid-messages) in this [supergroup](https://core.telegram.org/api/channel) or [monoforum](https://core.telegram.org/api/monoforum).
* Added [users.getRequirementsToContact](https://core.telegram.org/method/users.getRequirementsToContact) - Check whether we can write to the specified users, used to implement bulk checks for [Premium-only messages »](https://core.telegram.org/api/privacy#require-premium-for-new-non-contact-users) and [paid messages »](https://core.telegram.org/api/paid-messages).
* Added [payments.toggleStarGiftsPinnedToTop](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.toggleStarGiftsPinnedToTop) - Pins a received gift on top of the profile of the user or owned channels by using [payments.toggleStarGiftsPinnedToTop](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.toggleStarGiftsPinnedToTop).

##### Changed Methods

* Added **allow_paid_stars** parameter in [messages.sendMessage](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.sendMessage)
* Added **allow_paid_stars** parameter in [messages.sendMedia](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.sendMedia)
* Added **allow_paid_stars** parameter in [messages.forwardMessages](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.forwardMessages)
* Added **allow_paid_stars** parameter in [messages.sendInlineBotResult](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.sendInlineBotResult)
* Added **allow_paid_stars** parameter in [messages.sendMultiMedia](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.sendMultiMedia)
* Added **flags**, **for_paid_reactions** parameters in [channels.getSendAs](https://core.telegram.org/method/channels.getSendAs)
* Changed type of **private** from **flags.0?Bool** to **flags.0?PaidReactionPrivacy** in [messages.sendPaidReaction](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.sendPaidReaction)
* Changed type of **private** from **Bool** to **PaidReactionPrivacy** in [messages.togglePaidReactionPrivacy](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.togglePaidReactionPrivacy)

##### Deleted Methods

* Removed users.getIsPremiumRequiredToContact

##### New Constructors

* Added [paidReactionPrivacyDefault](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/paidReactionPrivacyDefault) - Uses the default reaction privacy, set using [messages.togglePaidReactionPrivacy](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.togglePaidReactionPrivacy).
* Added [paidReactionPrivacyAnonymous](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/paidReactionPrivacyAnonymous) - Send paid reactions anonymously.
* Added [paidReactionPrivacyPeer](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/paidReactionPrivacyPeer) - Send paid reactions as the specified peer, fetched using [channels.getSendAs](https://core.telegram.org/method/channels.getSendAs) with the `for_paid_reactions` flag set.
* Added [inputPrivacyKeyNoPaidMessages](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputPrivacyKeyNoPaidMessages) - Who can send you messages without paying, if [paid messages »](https://core.telegram.org/api/paid-messages) are enabled.
* Added [privacyKeyNoPaidMessages](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/privacyKeyNoPaidMessages) - Who can send you messages without paying, if [paid messages »](https://core.telegram.org/api/paid-messages) are enabled.
* Added [account.paidMessagesRevenue](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/account.paidMessagesRevenue) - Total number of non-refunded [Telegram Stars](https://core.telegram.org/api/stars) a user has spent on sending us messages either directly or through a channel, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/paid-messages) for more info on paid messages.
* Added [requirementToContactEmpty](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/requirementToContactEmpty) - This user can be freely contacted.
* Added [requirementToContactPremium](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/requirementToContactPremium) - This user requires us to buy a [Premium](https://core.telegram.org/api/premium) subscription in order to contact them.
* Added [requirementToContactPaidMessages](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/requirementToContactPaidMessages) - This user requires us to pay the specified amount of [Telegram Stars](https://core.telegram.org/api/stars) to send them a message, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/paid-messages) for the full flow.
* Added [inputInvoicePremiumGiftStars](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputInvoicePremiumGiftStars) - Used to gift a [Telegram Premium](https://core.telegram.org/api/premium) subscription to another user, paying with [Telegram Stars](https://core.telegram.org/api/stars).

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **paid_message_stars** parameter in [message](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/message)
* Added **charge_paid_message_stars**, **registration_month**, **phone_country**, **name_change_date**, **photo_change_date** parameters in [peerSettings](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/peerSettings)
* Added **send_paid_messages_stars** parameter, removed **premium_gifts** parameter in [userFull](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/userFull)
* Added **send_paid_messages_stars** parameter in [user](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/user)
* Added **send_paid_messages_stars** parameter in [channel](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/channel)
* Added **paid_messages_available** parameter in [channelFull](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/channelFull)
* Added **noncontact_peers_paid_stars** parameter in [globalPrivacySettings](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/globalPrivacySettings)
* Added **business_transfer**, **stargift_resale**, **paid_messages**, **premium_gift_months** parameters in [starsTransaction](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starsTransaction)
* Changed type of **private** from **Bool** to **PaidReactionPrivacy** in [updatePaidReactionPrivacy](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/updatePaidReactionPrivacy)
* Added **gift_address** parameter in [starGiftUnique](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftUnique)

##### Deleted Constructors

* Removed premiumGiftOption

### [Layer 198](https://core.telegram.org/schema?layer=198)

This layer introduces:

* [Collectibles as emoji statuses (and minor tweaks to the emoji status API) »](https://core.telegram.org/api/emoji-status#collectibles-as-emoji-statuses)
* Send gifts to channels
* [Notifications for gifts received by channels »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#notifications-for-received-channel-gifts)
* [Get all gifts received by a peer »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#list-all-received-gifts)
* [Fetch info about specific gifts owned by a peer we control »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#list-specific-owned-gifts)
* [Withdraw a collectible gift to the TON blockchain »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#withdraw-a-collectible-gift-to-the-ton-blockchain)
* [Custom video covers »](https://core.telegram.org/api/files#video-covers)
* [Custom starting timestamps for videos »](https://core.telegram.org/api/files#start-video-at-timestamp)

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [account.getCollectibleEmojiStatuses](https://core.telegram.org/method/account.getCollectibleEmojiStatuses) - Obtain a list of [emoji statuses »](https://core.telegram.org/api/emoji-status) for owned or [hosted collectible gifts »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#hosted-collectible-gifts).
* Added [payments.getSavedStarGifts](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.getSavedStarGifts) - Fetch the full list of [gifts »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#list-all-received-gifts) owned, received or [hosted »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#hosted-collectible-gifts) by a peer.
* Added [payments.getSavedStarGift](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.getSavedStarGift) - Fetch info about specific [gifts](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts) owned by a peer we control.
* Added [payments.getStarGiftWithdrawalUrl](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.getStarGiftWithdrawalUrl) - Convert a [collectible gift »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts) to an NFT on the TON blockchain.
* Added [payments.toggleChatStarGiftNotifications](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.toggleChatStarGiftNotifications) - Enables or disables the reception of notifications every time a [gift »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts) is received by the specified channel, can only be invoked by admins with `post_messages` [admin rights](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/chatAdminRights).

##### Changed Methods

* Added **video_timestamp** parameter in [messages.forwardMessages](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.forwardMessages)
* Added **stargift** parameter, removed **msg_id** parameter in [payments.saveStarGift](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.saveStarGift)
* Added **stargift** parameter, removed **msg_id** parameter in [payments.convertStarGift](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.convertStarGift)
* Added **stargift** parameter, removed **msg_id** parameter in [payments.upgradeStarGift](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.upgradeStarGift)
* Added **stargift** parameter, removed **msg_id** parameter, changed type of **to_id** from **InputUser** to **InputPeer** in [payments.transferStarGift](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.transferStarGift)

##### Deleted Methods

* Removed payments.getUserStarGifts
* Removed payments.getUserStarGift

##### New Constructors

* Added [emojiStatusCollectible](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/emojiStatusCollectible) - An owned or [hosted collectible gift »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#hosted-collectible-gifts) as emoji status.
* Added [inputEmojiStatusCollectible](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputEmojiStatusCollectible) - An owned or [hosted collectible gift »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#hosted-collectible-gifts) as emoji status: can only be used in [account.updateEmojiStatus](https://core.telegram.org/method/account.updateEmojiStatus), is never returned by the API.
* Added [savedStarGift](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/savedStarGift) - Represents a [gift](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts) owned by a peer.
* Added [payments.savedStarGifts](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/payments.savedStarGifts) - Represents a list of [gifts](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts).
* Added [inputSavedStarGiftUser](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputSavedStarGiftUser) - A gift received in a private chat with another user.
* Added [inputSavedStarGiftChat](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputSavedStarGiftChat) - A gift received by a channel we own.
* Added [payments.starGiftWithdrawalUrl](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/payments.starGiftWithdrawalUrl) - A URL that can be used to import the exported NFT on Fragment.

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **video_cover**, **video_timestamp** parameters in [inputMediaUploadedDocument](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputMediaUploadedDocument)
* Added **video_cover**, **video_timestamp** parameters in [inputMediaDocument](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputMediaDocument)
* Added **video_cover**, **video_timestamp** parameters in [messageMediaDocument](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageMediaDocument)
* Added **stargifts_available**, **stargifts_count** parameters in [channelFull](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/channelFull)
* Added **video_cover**, **video_timestamp** parameters in [inputMediaDocumentExternal](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputMediaDocumentExternal)
* Added **flags**, **until** parameters in [emojiStatus](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/emojiStatus)
* Added **peer** parameter, removed **user_id** parameter in [inputInvoiceStarGift](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputInvoiceStarGift)
* Added **from_id**, **peer**, **saved_id** parameters in [messageActionStarGift](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionStarGift)
* Changed type of **sender_id** from **flags.0?long** to **flags.0?Peer**, **recipient_id** from **long** to **Peer** in [starGiftAttributeOriginalDetails](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftAttributeOriginalDetails)
* Added **owner_address** parameter, changed type of **owner_id** from **flags.0?long** to **flags.0?Peer** in [starGiftUnique](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftUnique)
* Added **from_id**, **peer**, **saved_id** parameters in [messageActionStarGiftUnique](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messageActionStarGiftUnique)
* Added **stargift** parameter, removed **msg_id** parameter in [inputInvoiceStarGiftUpgrade](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputInvoiceStarGiftUpgrade)
* Added **stargift** parameter, removed **msg_id** parameter, changed type of **to_id** from **InputUser** to **InputPeer** in [inputInvoiceStarGiftTransfer](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/inputInvoiceStarGiftTransfer)

##### Deleted Constructors

* Removed emojiStatusUntil
* Removed userStarGift
* Removed payments.userStarGifts

### [Layer 197](https://core.telegram.org/schema?layer=197)

This layer introduces:

* [Collectible gift links »](https://core.telegram.org/api/links#collectible-gift-link)
* [Collectible gift story media areas »](https://core.telegram.org/api/stories#collectible-gifts)
* [Collectible webpage previews »](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/webPageAttributeUniqueStarGift)
* [Similar bot recommendations »](https://core.telegram.org/api/recommend#similar-bots)

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema) changes

##### New Methods

* Added [bots.getBotRecommendations](https://core.telegram.org/method/bots.getBotRecommendations) - Obtain a list of similarly themed bots, selected based on similarities in their subscriber bases, see [here »](https://core.telegram.org/api/recommend) for more info.
* Added [payments.getUniqueStarGift](https://core.telegram.org/method/payments.getUniqueStarGift) - Obtain info about a [collectible gift »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gifts) using a slug obtained from a [collectible gift link »](https://core.telegram.org/api/links#collectible-gift-link).

##### Changed Methods

* Changed type of [messages.getWebPagePreview](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.getWebPagePreview) from **MessageMedia** to **messages.WebPagePreview**

##### New Constructors

* Added [users.users](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/users.users) - Describes a list of users (or bots).
* Added [users.usersSlice](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/users.usersSlice) - Describes a partial list of users.
* Added [payments.uniqueStarGift](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/payments.uniqueStarGift) - Represents a [collectible gift »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gifts).
* Added [webPageAttributeUniqueStarGift](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/webPageAttributeUniqueStarGift) - Contains info about [collectible gift »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gifts) for a [webPage](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/webPage) preview of a [collectible gift »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gifts) (the [webPage](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/webPage) will have a `type` of `telegram_nft`).
* Added [mediaAreaStarGift](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/mediaAreaStarGift) - Represents a [collectible gift »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gifts).
* Added [messages.webPagePreview](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/messages.webPagePreview) - Represents a webpage preview.

##### Changed Constructors

* Added **flags**, **slug**, **owner_name** parameters, changed type of **owner_id** from **long** to **flags.0?long** in [starGiftUnique](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/starGiftUnique)

#### [Schema](https://core.telegram.org/schema)

```
starGiftUnique#3482f322 flags:# id:long title:string slug:string num:int owner_id:flags.0?long owner_name:flags.1?string attributes:Vector<StarGiftAttribute> availability_issued:int availability_total:int = StarGift;  

users.users#62d706b8 users:Vector<User> = users.Users;
users.usersSlice#315a4974 count:int users:Vector<User> = users.Users;  

payments.uniqueStarGift#caa2f60b gift:StarGift users:Vector<User> = payments.UniqueStarGift;  

webPageAttributeUniqueStarGift#cf6f6db8 gift:StarGift = WebPageAttribute;  

mediaAreaStarGift#5787686d coordinates:MediaAreaCoordinates slug:string = MediaArea;
messages.webPagePreview#b53e8b21 media:MessageMedia users:Vector<User> = messages.WebPagePreview;  

---functions---  

messages.getWebPagePreview#570d6f6f flags:# message:string entities:flags.3?Vector<MessageEntity> = messages.WebPagePreview;  

bots.getBotRecommendations#a1b70815 bot:InputUser = users.Users;  

payments.getUniqueStarGift#a1974d72 slug:string = payments.UniqueStarGift;
```

### [Layer 196](https://core.telegram.org/schema?layer=196)

This layer introduces:

* [Extra secure group calls »](https://telegram.org/blog/group-calls-made-easy)
* [Explicit delivery acknowledgement for Telegram Gateway verification messages »](https://core.telegram.org/method/messages.reportMessagesDelivery).
* [Third-party verification »](https://core.telegram.org/api/bots/verification)
* [Reactions for service messages »](https://core.telegram.org/api/reactions)
* [Collectible gifts »](https://core.telegram.org/api/gifts#collectible-gifts)
* Add support for entities in folder titles, and a new `title_noanimate` flag to optionally freeze animated emoji entities in the title.
* All flags added to [channel](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/channel) (listed below) are valid `min` flags, meaning they must be applied over the locally stored version even if the `min` flag of the incoming [channel](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/channel) is set.
* All flags added to [user](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/user) (listed below) are valid `min` flags, meaning they must be applied over the locally stored version even if the `min` flag of the incoming [user](https://core.telegram.org/constructor/user) is set.

#### PUSH notification changes

##### New PUSH notifications

* Added [MESSAGE_STARGIFT_UPGRADE](https://core.telegram.org/api/push-updates#message-stargift-upgrade) - `{1} upgraded your Gift`
* Added [MESSAGE_UNIQUE_STARGIFT](https://core.telegram.org/api/push-updates#message-unique-stargift) - `{1} transferred you a Gift!`
